



Shopping with Walmart




The Walmart Site and App Experience


Order everything from groceries to gadgets using Walmart.com or the Walmart app. Take advantage of the new features that make shopping simpler while saving you time and money.

How to get your items
You have three ways to order your items:

Pickup: Pickup your order curbside at a nearby store.
Delivery: We'll bring your order to your home from a store. (Fees may apply)
Shipping: Your order will be shipped fast via UPS, FedEx, or another carrier.
Pickup and Delivery from store
Select a nearby store and timeslot to pick up your items, or we can deliver your order right to your home.

How it works:

Sign into your Walmart account.
On the homepage, select How do you want your items?
Under How do you want your items select Pickup or Delivery.
Select Delivery to check to see if your address can receive Delivery services.
If your address is within range of a store that offers Delivery services, the website or app will list those stores. You can choose a store from the list and then select Save As My Store.
If no stores are listed, the address is not within range of Delivery services from a store. You must choose Shipping or Pickup to place an order.
Reserve a Pickup/Delivery time.
Add items to your order.
Not all items are available for Pickup and Delivery.
Select Checkout under the Pickup and delivery options section of your cart.
Select Checkout all items to place an order for all items, both Pickup/Delivery and shipping.
Check out by the time displayed to keep your reservation.
If you order an item that isn’t available for pickup and delivery from your local store, we’ll ship it to you. We group shipped items separately in your cart and they ship separately from your reserved pickup or delivery order. Look for availability info on the item page and search results page.

Find what you need
Use search to find and find items, including groceries, and filter by how and when you want them.

How it works:

Sign into your Walmart account.
Enter an item in the Search bar at the top of your screen.
On the Search results page, filter by how and when you want to get items:
Need it fast: use this filter to show items that are available for your time preference.
Pickup & shipping: use this filter to show just the items that are available for your preference.
Pickup, Delivery and Shipping Fees
Fees apply to some methods, and you can see any fees from your cart.

Pickup: Pick up orders over $35 for free. Orders under the $35 minimum have a below order minimum fee.
Standard Delivery from store: $9.95 delivery fee applies.
Delivery is free for Walmart+ members for orders over $35.
Learn about Walmart+
Shipping: Orders under the $35 order minimum have a below order minimum fee of $6.99.
We waive the fee for Walmart+ customers.
Express delivery: $10 Express fee applies to all orders in addition to the $9.95 standard delivery fee.
We waive the standard delivery fee for Walmart+ members.
Learn about Walmart+
3 hr. or less delivery: $6 fee applies to all orders.
$9.95 standard delivery fee applies.
Drone delivery: $19.99 delivery fee applies to all drone delivery orders.
Walmart+ members pay zero drone delivery fees.
Learn about Drone Delivery
Extra Shipping fees:
Freight Items (items that are heavy or oversized) have separate surcharges.
Marketplace items may have separate shipping charges but count toward the $35 minimum amount for free shipping from Walmart.
Pharmacy, photo, wireless and tires don't count toward the $35 minimum amount and don't ship for free.
APO/FPO military addresses may have separate charges.
Hawaii, Alaska, US Territories, and Overseas Locations: Items that Walmart sells and ships have added shipping fees when we ship to these locations.
You can check the shipping fees for the item by adding it to the cart. There will be a separate line in the Payments section for this fee.
Bag fees: Certain states or local municipalities may charge bag fees to encourage the use of reusable bags. You can visit Single-Use Bag Policy to learn more.
Colorado (CO) Retail Delivery Fee: There is an added retail delivery fee of $0.28 for all deliveries by motor vehicle in Colorado. We charge this delivery fee within the state of CO for any item(s) that are subject to sales tax or use tax. For more details, you can visit Temporary Holds and Charges.
Mobile Money Services
Walmart offers money services in-store. Visit your store’s Money Center or Customer Service counter for these services:

Bill Pay
Money Transfers
Cash Checks
Money Services are not yet available in the app.



Find, Purchase and Send a Gift


You can easily send gifts to family and friends from Walmart.com

We send an email to your gift recipient that includes your personalized gift message and digital receipt. We'll itemize the receipt and won't include the price of the gift.
For pickup orders, you'll receive an email, which includes a gift receipt you can share with the recipient.
Find gift-eligible items
Follow these steps to find items eligible for gifting:

On the search results page, under the filter Gifting, select the Gift eligible checkbox.
Select an item you would like to gift.
On the item page, you can confirm that the item is gift eligible by finding the gift icon and the label This item is gift eligible below the shipment options.
Some items may have a Packaging Note below the eligibility label.
Ships in the manufacturer's original packaging, which may reveal the contents.
Add the gift to your cart.
Will the gift arrive in a Walmart box?

Shipped from Walmart.com:
Will be packaged in a Walmart box when possible.
An item can’t be packaged in a Walmart box will be shipped in its original manufacturer box, which may reveal the contents.
Delivery from store and pickup items: no, these items won’t be packaged in a Walmart box, which may reveal the contents.
Checkout with gift-eligible items
In cart, check the box labeled This order is a gift.
Confirm pickup and delivery options. These options differ between delivery and pickup.
Delivery orders with gift-eligible items

Enter and confirm the recipient's delivery address.
Enter and confirm the recipient's email address.
You may enter your own email address if you don't know the recipient's email. However, this means the recipient won't receive your customized gift message, which is the only way they'll know who gave them a gift.
Add a customized gift message.
Select a payment method.
Review and place the order.
We’ll send two emails to the email address you gave us for the recipient.

The first email includes:

Estimated delivery date
Gifter's name
Tracking details (we won't reveal the prices or items)
The second email includes:

Gifter's name
Gift details
Instructions on returns
Link to start a return
Gift receipt (we'll itemize the receipt and won't include the price of the gift)
Pickup orders with gift-eligible items

Only the person placing the order will receive emails.
No recipient emails or gift message.
When you place the order, you’ll receive a confirmation once the order is picked up. This email includes the gift receipt with instructions on returns.
You can share the email or gift receipt with the person you're gifting.



Out of Stock Items


If an item isn’t available, it will show out-of-stock.

Unfortunately, sometimes an item may show available when you place an order, but by the time we process the order, the item is no longer in stock. If this happens, we'll email you to let you know that we canceled the item or the order. We restock items as quickly as we can.



Quantity Limits and Bulk Purchases



Some items have limits on the amount we can ship to you. If you can't add more of an item to your cart, it's either due to quantity limits or stock availability. Unfortunately, we can't send more than the quantity limit allows.

Walmart.com reserves the right to limit the quantity of items purchased per person, household, or order. These restrictions may apply to orders placed by the same Walmart account; using the same credit card, gift card, and/or checking account; and orders that use the same billing and/or shipping address.

Our website is meant for personal, non-commercial use. Since our items are already at everyday low prices, we generally don't offer a discount on bulk purchases. We reserve the right to prohibit dealers or resellers.



Price and Other Listing Errors



Listing errors are incorrect details about the product in item listings.

Some of the product details that may contain errors are:

The item’s price
The images or description (color, size, features, etc.)
Quantity or what’s included
If we catch a listing error while processing your order, we'll cancel your order.

Canceling your order is the last thing we want to do, but it may be necessary when we find listings with incorrect details.
If we need to cancel your order, we'll send you an email with the reason for cancellation.
We reserve the right to cancel item(s) and orders in accordance with our Terms of Use.



Extra Savings


At Walmart, we believe our Every Day Low Prices are key to saving customers money so they can live better. Check out the benefits of our Walmart+ program for more ways to save.

Some Marketplace sellers may offer extra savings on select items.

On eligible items, you’ll see a special banner.

For items with a special banner that says Save an extra $(amount):

Select the checkbox on the banner to use the discount.
To see the eligible savings amount and select the checkbox, you must be signed in.
The seller may limit the quantity you can purchase with a discount. If you’ve reached the limit, the banner will say Savings redeemed.

For items with a special banner than says Buy (quantity), save $(amount):

Add the stated amount of eligible items from that Marketplace seller to your cart.
Select the View other eligible items link in the banner to see and select more eligible items from that seller. You may be able to mix and match eligible items offering the same savings when they are sold by the same seller.
You’ll see the discount split between the items.
Example:
For Buy 2, save $9:
Item 1 may originally be $10, but show as $7 (a savings of $3).
Item 2 may originally be $20, but show as $14 (a savings of $6).
The total will reflect a discount of $9 savings when 2 eligible items are selected.
You'll see the eligible discounts in the cart and they will be applied at checkout.
For information about using printed manufacturer coupons, please review our Coupon Policy.

Please note that all savings outlined are subject to the Walmart Terms of Use. For further details, please view the Walmart Terms of Use.



Store or Associate Feedback


Did you have an experience in a Walmart Store or Neighborhood Market Store that you’d like to share? Your opinion is important to us, and we want to hear from you.

Provide us with feedback about your in-store experience:
Visit The Corporate Feedback Form.
Choose Store Experience from the Topic list.
Select Continue.
Give as much detail as you'd like in the Enter your Feedback section.
Enter the required contact information under Tell us a little about yourself section.
Select Continue.
Choose the store that your feedback is for.
If you can't find the store, you can search for it by zip code, address, or contact Customer Care using the Chat with us button.
Select Submit feedback to submit the form.



Food Safety


Walmart ensures the safety of our food through:

Maintaining clean facilities
We have routine sanitation schedules for all facilities and equipment.
We have our stores regularly inspected by local, state, and federal agencies and third party food safety auditors.
Associate training which includes:
Understanding food safety and quality.
Maintaining required standards for hygiene, cleanliness, cooking, proper temperature control, and proper bagging.
Bagging raw proteins in meat bags separately from other foods. This aims to prevent cross-contamination.
Bagging chemicals separately from all foods.
Time and temperature requirements for food safety.
Proper product management
For recalled products, we:
Work quickly to block the item from selling.
Remove it from our store’s shelves (including pickup & delivery).
Visit our corporate site for more information about how recalled products impact our facilities.

Food Safety Tips
To keep your food safe and fresh:

Place all chilled and/or frozen products in the refrigerator or freezer as soon as possible.
If using contactless delivery, retrieve and stow your order as soon as possible.
Place raw meat and seafood on the bottom shelf.
Avoid direct contact of raw meats/seafood with ready-to-eat foods.
Always wash whole produce thoroughly before you eat it.
Thoroughly cook foods to a recommended, safe internal temperature.
Place any leftovers in the refrigerator within two hours of cooking.
Date Labeling
Best If Used By: This date shows when the item is at peak quality (as suggested by the product supplier/manufacturer).
While it's not an expiration date, it’s a good indication of the quality of the product.
Use By or Freeze By: This date shows a product that is time or temperature-sensitive.
You should either use or place the product in your freezer by this date.
Fresh-sliced Deli Labels
You may see today’s date on a fresh-sliced deli meat or cheese item. This is the date the meat or cheese was sliced, not an expiration date.



Online Vision Center Orders


You can now order glasses and frames on Walmart.com and contacts on Walmartcontacts.com! We ship the items to your home, and they arrive within 7-14 business days.

Placing an Order
Login into your Walmart.com account.
Search for eyeglasses and add your frames to the cart.
Select Options to customize.
Selecting eligible frames will show the prompt Make these frames your own.
Select the prescription type, materials, and lens add-ons.
Review your selection and select Add prescription.
You'll see a prompt to sign in (if needed) or a prompt to create a new Vision profile (if the prescription is for someone else).
Enter your pupillary distance (PD).
This is the distance in millimeters between the centers of your pupils.
You can enter it manually or select Measure Now to have your camera measure it for you.
Select Add to cart.
You can continue adding items until you're ready to check out.
Check out.
You'll get a confirmation email when you place the order and another when it ships.
Returns and Refunds
You can return your glasses in the Walmart app. They're eligible for refund to your original payment method only within the 60-day return window. They're not eligible for replacement.

Go to the Order Details page.
Select Start a Return.
Select the item(s) to return.
Select the item issue and item condition.
Select the return method
Review the order and select Submit.
Common Questions
What information do I need to place a Vision order?

You need a valid, current prescription and a PD measurement. Prescription requirements vary by state. You can measure your PD on Walmart.com.

Can I use a prescription for contact lenses to order glasses?

Contact lenses and glasses lenses are different prescriptions. See Walmart Contacts for more information.

How will I be notified if there's a problem with my prescription or order?

You'll get an email or text within 48 hours letting you know that the order has canceled.

Can I order progressive or bifocal lenses?

No, only single-vision lenses are available at this time. Progressive and bifocal lenses will be available in the future.

What if I have questions about a frame fit or style?

Please contact your local Walmart Vision Center for help.

What if my frames need adjustment?

Please visit your local Walmart Vision Center for free adjustments.

Can I use insurance benefits or insurance reimbursement?

We don't accept insurance for online eyewear orders at this time. We do accept most major insurance in the Vision Centers. Please contact your local Walmart Vision Center or your insurance provider for more information.

How can I find my order confirmation or track my order?

View your order in your purchase history or use the Walmart app to track your package.

Is there a warranty on my frames?

There is a 60-day satisfaction guarantee with online Vision orders. This doesn't apply to eyewear from other departments, such as Sporting Goods or Apparel/Accessories. You can also buy a 12-month replacement guarantee at a Walmart Vision Center within 14 days of your purchase. The guarantee isn't available in:

Florida
Puerto Rico



Apple Services Offer


The Apple Services Offer is no longer available when buying qualifying items in stores and is now only available when purchased online. If you purchased qualifying items in stores before this change, you can claim these offers until July 2026.

Enjoy a world of entertainment from Apple. When you buy select items on Walmart.com, or the Walmart app, you may be eligible for Apple Service free trials which could include:

Apple TV+ (up to 3 months)
Apple Music (up to 4 months)
Apple Fitness+ (up to 3 months)
Apple Arcade (up to 4 months)
Apple News (up to 3 months)
Apple Books (up to 3 books)
Eligibility
This offer is open to all Walmart customers, but there are restrictions.

You must:

Buy a qualifying item on Walmart.com or the Walmart app.
Be new to the Apple Service you're trying to sign up for or have an expired account.
Example: You wouldn’t be eligible for a free trial of Apple Music if you’re a current Apple Music subscriber.
You can check your current Apple services subscriptions on your iOS device by going to:
Settings > Apple ID > Subscriptions

If you’re unsure, contact Apple Support to confirm if you’re eligible for the free trials.

How It Works
In-Store

If you purchased a qualifying product in a Walmart store before April 15, 2025, you’ll see a code on your receipt. Use the code at www.wmt-offer.com to get your Apple Services free trial.
Online

When you buy a qualifying product from Walmart.com or the Walmart app, you’ll see a code on your Order Details page and in the confirmation email.
You’ll only see the code after you receive your order.
Select Redeem Now and follow the prompts to get your Apple Services free trial.
Troubleshooting
Contact Apple Support﻿ for all issues with Apple Services.
If you bought an eligible item online and didn’t receive a code, check your email and Order Details page in your Purchase History.
If you still don’t have the code, or the code isn’t working, select the Chat with Us button at the bottom of the page to contact Customer Care.
Lost Receipt
If you paid with a credit card in the store and lost your receipt, you may be able to find it using the Receipt Lookup Tool.



Registry



Spark Good Registry


The Spark Good Registry lets you support your favorite charities in two ways:

Donate change by rounding up your purchase during checkout on Walmart.com.
Shop a wish list from a nonprofit’s registry and have the items shipped straight to them.
How to Find a Spark Good Registry
From the Walmart.com website or app:

Select Registry.
Website: look at the menu across the top of the page.
App: look at the menu across the bottom of the screen.
Go to the Find a Registry section and select Support a local cause or find a nonprofit organization.
Go to the Find a local charity section and:
Search for a charity or nonprofit by location, type, or cause.
Select a charity or nonprofit from the list.



Managing a Gift Registry or Wishlist


Managing a Gift Registry
From Walmart.com or the Walmart app:

Select Registry from the menu at the top of the page.
You can also select Services > Registry, Lists, & Gifts > Registries.
Select the Manage now button under Manage your registry.
Select the registry you want to manage.
Select the Manage button.
Select the pencil in the Walmart app.
You can make changes to the registry information including:

Name
Registry Name
Event Date
Location
Make the registry public
Selecting the box to make your registry public allows people to search for it.
Shipping Address
Select the Save button when you finish making changes. Select the Delete this registry link to delete the registry.

You can share your registry with friends and family. To share your registry:

Select Registry.
Select Manage Now under Manage your registry.
Select your registry.
Select the Share button.
Select how you’d like to share your wishlist.
Copy URL
Facebook
Twitter
Email
Managing a Wishlist
From Walmart.com or the Walmart app:

Select My Items.
Select the Lists.
Select the Wishlist you want to manage.
Select the Manage button.
Select the pencil in the Walmart app.
You can make changes to the wishlist information including:

Wishlist Name
Event Date
Location
Shipping Address
Select the Save button when you finish making changes. Select the Delete this wishlist link to delete the wishlist.

You can share your wishlist, but friends and family can’t search for it like a gift registry.

To share your wishlist:

Select My Items.
Select Lists.
Select your wishlist.
Select the Share button.
Select how you’d like to share your wishlist
Copy URL
Facebook
Twitter
Email



Adding and Managing Gift Registry or Wishlist Items


Adding Items
You can add items to a registry or Wishlist:

In-store
On the app or Walmart.com
From the registry or wishlist page
In-Store

You must have the Walmart app on your mobile device to add items in-store. When you find an in-store item you want to add:

Open the Walmart app.
Select the scan icon in the search box.
Scan the UPC barcode on the item.
Select Add to Registry or Add to Wishlist on the item page.
Select the registry or wishlist you want to add the item to.
Select Save.
On the app or Walmart.com

Go to an item page.
Select the Add to Registry or Add to Wishlist button.
Select the registry or wishlist you want to add the item to.
Select Save.
From the Registry Page

Go to the registry landing page.
Select the Explore tab.
Select the Checklist tab to explore categories of popular items.
Scroll to view popular items and categories.
Select the Add to Registry button.
Items you’ve added will show Added to Registry.

From the Wishlist Page

Go to the Wishlist landing page.
Select the Explore tab.
Scroll to view categories and popular items.
Select an item to go to the item page.
Select the Add to Wishlist button.
Items you’ve added will show as Added to Wishlist.

Managing and Removing Items
Gift registry and wishlist owners can modify items and quantities or mark something as purchased.

Gift Registries

From Walmart.com or the Walmart app:

Select Registry at the top of the page.
Select the Manage now button under Manage your registry.
Select the gift registry you want to manage.
Find the item you want to manage in the registry.
Select the Remove link to remove the item from your registry.

Select the Manage link to update the quantity needed or to mark the item as purchased.

Wishlists

From Walmart.com or the Walmart app:

Select My Items at the top of the page.
Select Lists.
Select the wishlist you want to manage.
Find the item you want to manage in the wishlist.
Select the Remove link to remove the item from your wishlist.

Select the Manage link to update the quantity needed or to mark the item as purchased.



Creating a Gift Registry or Wishlist


Gift registries and wishlists let event organizers create a list of needed gifts/items for an event or occasion. You can create the following types of gift registries at Walmart:

Baby
Wedding
Classroom
Spark Good
Wishlist
Any Occasion registries are now Wishlists.
Gift registries expire after 2 years of inactivity.

Creating a Registry
Sign into Walmart.com
Select Registry from the menu at the top of the page.
You can also select Services > Registry, Lists, & Gifts > Registries.
Select the Manage now button under Manage your registry.
Select the Create a registry button.
Select the type of registry you want to create:
Baby
Wedding
Classroom
Spark Good Registry
Enter the details.
Select the event date.
Select where you’d like to receive the gifts for shipped items.
Select whether the registry is public or private.
Private registries won’t show up in searches. You’ll have to share the link with family and friends.
Add items to your registry.
See Adding Items to a Registry or Wishlist.
Creating a Wishlist
Wishlists let you create a list of gift ideas for holidays, birthdays, or any occasion. You can share your wishlist with friends and family members. Friends and family members can purchase items on the list, mark them as purchased, and have the item shipped directly to you.

From the Walmart app or walmart.com:

Select My Items.
Select Lists.
Select the Create a wishlist button.
Give your wishlist a name.
Add the date of the event.
Add items to your wishlist.
See Adding Items to a Registry or Wishlist.﻿



Marking an Item as Purchased for Gift Givers


You can mark a gift registry or wishlist item as purchased without contacting the registry or wishlist owner.

Marking an Item as Purchased
From Walmart.com or the Walmart app:

Go to the Registry or Wishlist.
See Finding a Gift Registry.﻿
Select the wishlist link from the wishlist owner.
Find the item you purchased.
Select Mark as Purchased.
Enter the details:
Purchased quantity
Who the gift is from
Where you purchased the item.
Select Mark as Purchased to complete the entry.
Where You Purchased the Item
You don’t have to purchase gift registry or wishlist items from Walmart. You can select other retailers from a drop-down menu when marking the item as purchased.

If you choose Walmart, you can enter the TC number from your in-store receipt or your walmart.com order number.

TC Number

The TC number is a 16-digit number on the receipt near the barcode.

Walmart.com Order Number

The order number is in your purchase history.

20. Disputes, Arbitration, and Applicable Law
PLEASE READ THIS SECTION CAREFULLY. IT MAY SIGNIFICANTLY AFFECT YOUR LEGAL RIGHTS, INCLUDING YOUR RIGHT TO FILE A LAWSUIT IN COURT.

Arbitration Agreement.
EXCEPT FOR DISPUTES THAT QUALIFY FOR SMALL CLAIMS COURT, YOU AGREE THAT ALL DISPUTES ARISING OUT OF OR RELATED TO THESE TERMS OF USE OR ANY ASPECT OF THE RELATIONSHIP BETWEEN YOU AND WALMART, INCLUDING ANY PRODUCTS OR SERVICES OFFERED OR SOLD BY WALMART OR THE WALMART ENTITIES, WHETHER BASED IN CONTRACT, TORT, STATUTE, FRAUD, MISREPRESENTATION, OR ANY OTHER LEGAL THEORY, WILL BE RESOLVED THROUGH FINAL AND BINDING ARBITRATION BEFORE A SINGLE NEUTRAL ARBITRATOR INSTEAD OF IN A COURT BY A JUDGE OR JURY. EXCEPT AS OTHERWISE PROVIDED IN THESE TERMS OF USE, YOU AND WALMART AGREE THAT EACH IS WAIVING THE RIGHT TO SUE IN COURT AND TO HAVE A TRIAL BY A JURY. This paragraph may be referred to as the Arbitration Agreement in these Terms of Use.

Class Action and Mass Action Waiver.
YOU AND WALMART AGREE THAT ANY ARBITRATION REQUIRED BY THE ARBITRATION AGREEMENT WILL TAKE PLACE ON AN INDIVIDUAL BASIS. You and Walmart each agree to waive the right to have any dispute or claim subject to the Arbitration Agreement brought, heard, administered, resolved, or arbitrated as a class arbitration, class action, collective action, or Mass Action to the maximum extent permitted by law. “Mass Action” means a situation in which a party is represented by a law firm or other representative, or a collection of law firms or other representatives, that has initiated more than one hundred (100) arbitration Demands with common questions of law or fact against Walmart within 180 days of initiating your arbitration Demand. This paragraph may be referred to as the “Class Action and Mass Action Waiver” in these Terms of Use. Nothing in these Terms of Use precludes you from bringing issues to the attention of federal, state, or local government agencies and, if the applicable law allows, such agencies may seek relief against Walmart for you.

Initiating a Demand for Arbitration.
Any arbitration required by the Arbitration Agreement shall be initiated by You or Walmart by sending the other a written demand for arbitration (“Demand”) only via first-class mail, FedEx, or UPS within the statute of limitations period. Your Demand shall be delivered to: Walmart Inc. Legal Department, 702 SW 8th Street, Mailstop 0215, Bentonville, AR 72716. The Demand must include (i) the name, telephone number, mailing address, and email address of the person or entity seeking arbitration; (ii) a statement of the legal claims asserted and the factual basis for those claims; (iii) a description of the remedy sought and an accurate, good-faith calculation of the amount in controversy; (iv) the original personal signature of the party seeking arbitration; and (v) the original personal signature of any legal counsel or other representative purporting to represent the party seeking arbitration. For purposes of this paragraph, “original personal signature” does not include any digital, scanned, electronic, copied, or facsimile signature. An original personal signature on the Demand certifies the following: (i) the Demand is not being presented for any improper purpose, such as to harass, cause unnecessary delay, or needlessly increase the cost of litigation; (ii) the claims and other legal contentions are warranted by existing law or by a non-frivolous argument for extending, modifying, or reversing existing law or for establishing new law; and (iii) the factual contentions have evidentiary support or, if specifically so identified, will likely have evidentiary support after a reasonable opportunity for further investigation or discovery. The original personal signature by the party seeking arbitration shall verify under penalty of perjury that the factual statements contained in the Demand are true and correct. Compliance with this paragraph tolls any applicable statute of limitations as to any dispute or claim subject to the Arbitration Agreement that is stated in the Demand.

Filing a Demand for Arbitration.
A party seeking arbitration of any dispute or claim subject to the Arbitration Agreement shall submit a copy of the Demand to the American Arbitration Association (“AAA”) sixty (60) or more days after the Demand is initiated pursuant to Section 20.3 of these Terms of Use. The arbitration will be administered by the AAA pursuant to the current AAA Consumer Arbitration Rules, except to the extent modified by these Terms of Use. The AAA rules and instructions are available on the AAA website at www.adr.org/consumer.

Conduct of Arbitration.
In any arbitration of a dispute or claim that is subject to the Arbitration Agreement:

You or Walmart may file such dispositive motions as would be permitted by the Federal Rules of Civil Procedure without obtaining permission from the arbitrator. Upon the filing of any dispositive motion, the arbitrator may stay all further action and deadlines in the arbitration until deciding such motion.
The parties shall have the right to conduct such discovery from the opposing party or any third party that is proportional to the needs of the claim or dispute, considering the importance of the issues at stake, the amount in controversy, the importance of the discovery in resolving the issues, and whether the burden or expense of the proposed discovery outweighs its likely benefit. Proportionality shall be decided by the arbitrator when requested by either party.
The arbitrator shall conduct any calls, conferences, or hearings by teleconference or videoconference, unless the arbitrator determines that an in-person hearing is appropriate. Any in-person hearing will be held at a location that is reasonably convenient. You and an employee of Walmart shall attend all arbitrator calls, conferences, and hearings.
A party may make a written offer of judgment at least seven (7) days before the date set for the arbitration hearing.
If the arbitrator finds that a party’s claim, counterclaim, or appeal was frivolous, asserted in bad faith, or pursued for purposes of harassment, the arbitrator shall award the opposing party its attorneys’ fees, costs, and expenses and all amounts charged by AAA for the arbitration.
The arbitrator will follow these Terms of Use and the law. The arbitrator shall not have the authority to commit errors of law or legal reasoning. The arbitrator may award relief only in favor of the individual party seeking relief and only to the extent necessary to provide relief warranted by that individual party’s claim. The arbitrator may not award relief for or against anyone who is not a party, though the individual relief awarded by the arbitrator may incidentally affect non-parties.
You or Walmart may appeal an arbitration award that manifestly disregards the law by filing a notice of appeal with the AAA within 30 days after an award is rendered and delivered to the parties. The appeal shall be heard by a single arbitrator unless the parties agree to a multi-arbitrator appellate panel. The arbitrator(s) assigned to the appeal shall be selected by agreement of the parties from among those on the AAA Appellate Panel. If the parties are unable to agree, AAA shall appoint the arbitrator(s) from among those on the AAA Appellate Panel. An arbitrator who previously presided over any aspect of a case shall be ineligible from serving as an appellate arbitrator in that same case. The appeal, including briefing, shall be conducted pursuant to the parties’ agreement or the appellate arbitrator(s)’ orders if the parties are unable to agree.
Process for Arbitration Bellwether Demands.
If twenty-five (25) or more Demands for arbitration of a dispute or claim subject to the Arbitration Agreement are initiated (i) that involve common questions of law or fact and (ii) where the initiating parties are represented by the same law firm, the same group of coordinated law firms, or the same representative, then such Demands shall be coordinated pursuant to this paragraph. The group of claimants and group of respondents shall each select five (5) Demands, for a total of ten (10), to be filed with AAA (collectively the “Bellwether Demands”). Each of the Bellwether Demands shall be assigned by AAA to a different arbitrator chosen from its national roster. After ninety (90) days from the date all Bellwether Demands have been arbitrated and any appeals exhausted, any remaining Demands subject to this paragraph may be filed with AAA. The AAA Supplementary Rules for Multiple Case Filings shall apply to the extent they are not inconsistent with this paragraph. Notwithstanding any other provision in the Terms of Use, You and Walmart agree that a court of competent jurisdiction shall have authority to enforce the terms of this paragraph with injunctive or other relief.

Severability.
If (i) any dispute or claim subject to the Arbitration Agreement is filed as a class arbitration, class action, collective action, representative action, or Mass Action and (ii) if the Class Action and Mass Action Waiver is held invalid or unenforceable as to such dispute or claim, then the Arbitration Agreement does not apply to that dispute or claim and such dispute or claim must be brought in a court of competent jurisdiction. If any provision of Section 20 other than the Class Action and Mass Action Waiver is held invalid or unenforceable as to a particular dispute or claim, then such provision shall be severed from the Terms of Use, and the remainder of the Terms of Use shall be enforceable to the maximum extent permitted by law.

Applicable Law.
The Federal Arbitration Act (“FAA”) governs the interpretation and enforcement of Section 20 of these Terms of Use. If the FAA is found not to apply to any portion of Section 20 of these Terms of Use, then the applicable laws of the State of Arkansas shall apply without regard to choice-of-law principles.

2.1 Walmart+ Free Shipping, No Order Minimum, and Free Delivery From Your Store Benefits.
Walmart+ free shipping, no order minimum (“FSNM”) and free delivery from your store (“Free Delivery”) benefits are dependent upon inventory availability, available delivery time slots, and in some cases, the delivery address. Walmart+ FSNM-eligible orders are limited to items sold by Walmart or shipped by Walmart, through Walmart.com or the Walmart App. It is not available for items sold and shipped by Marketplace Retailers. We may also exclude products with special delivery characteristics at our discretion. Walmart+ Free Delivery applies to items available for delivery from physical Walmart stores located in your specific delivery area, and for which you select a specific delivery time slot when your order is placed through Walmart.com or the Walmart App. Delivery addresses eligible for free delivery may change from time-to-time, without notice to you. A $35 minimum order applies. For more information about the details of Walmart+ FSNM and Free Delivery benefits and service, see FAQs, available at https://www.walmart.com/plus/faq.

The Walmart mobile scan & go features designed to allow Members to use their mobile devices in participating Walmart stores to scan items for purchase into a digital cart, and then to authorize payment for that cart. Any uses subject to the following additional terms:

Accessing and Using Scan and Go
You agree to accurately scan the barcode for all products you wish to purchase, before checkout, and to pay for all products you obtain from any Walmart store. If you are unable to scan any products for any reason (including due to lack of connectivity with your mobile service), you must consult with a Walmart associate to make sure all items are either reflected in your digital cart before checkout or are reflected on your physical or digital receipt after checkout.
Upon scanning the relevant barcode for a product, that product will appear in your digital cart on your mobile device, along with the price for that product. Upon scanning certain products, like some fruits and vegetables, your digital cart will only reflect an estimated price for the product. The price for such products will be finalized as part of the exit and payment process, where you will be asked to weigh or provide the item count for the product. As a result, the total payment amount reflected for your digital cart may change.
You agree that Walmart may review the products in your possession and/or your receipt or proof of purchase on your mobile device at any time (sometimes referred to as an “item check”), and that Walmart can require that any products be re-scanned or removed from your physical and/or digital cart, and that any product price or digital cart total be re-calculated. In the event you and any Walmart associate calculate different totals for your purchase, the calculation confirmed by the Walmart associate will be the amount payable by you.
In order to finalize your purchase and exit the store, before leaving the store, you are required and agree: (i) to proceed to any available self-checkout register, and then pair your mobile device with the self-checkout register by scanning the QR code displayed on the screen; (ii) after pairing your mobile device with a self-checkout register, to follow the instructions provided , which may include, among other things, weighing certain fruits and vegetables, providing proof of age in order to purchase age-restricted items, or submitting to an item check; and (iii) upon completion of all instructions in select your preferred payment method and complete your payment, You will receive a digital receipt of the transaction (or, in some cases, a physical receipt may be rendered to you from the self-checkout register), and you may exit the store.
In some Walmart locations where a self-checkout register is not currently enabled, or in other select circumstances, you may be required to complete a “digital cart transfer”, which transfers products scanned into your digital cart over to a self-checkout register or to a staffed register, where your transaction will be completed in the traditional manner (but without having to re-scan all of your items again at the register).
Subject to the laws of the jurisdiction of the store location in which you are shopping, you may be able to scan certain age-restricted items, like alcohol, into your digital cart provided you meet the minimum age requirements of that jurisdiction. Walmart may alter, suspend, or terminate use of Scan and Go at any time at its discretion without notice to you.
For certain large purchases, you will be asked to confirm your payment method and identity with a Walmart associate located at the store exit by presenting the credit card used for the transaction and a government-issued photo identification. If you are unable or unwilling to do so, you must pay for your purchase using traditional check-out methods.
We may elect to make certain items in our stores ineligible for purchase through the SNG App at any time, in our sole discretion, with no prior notice to you (for example, firearms, ammunition, alcohol, tobacco, gift cards that are not pre-activated, tires, car batteries, hearing center items, R-rated movies and games, prescriptions and some harsh chemicals).

For Delivery Personnel safety and your safety, the Delivery Personnel will not stop the recorded video footage at any point during delivery.  You may view the Recordings of our Delivery Personnel taken by our Delivery Personnel’s camera either in real time or at a later time via the Walmart App. You will not have the ability to speak to the Delivery Personnel via the Walmart App once they are in the Home; however, at any time you may contact InHome Customer Support who will contact the Delivery Personnel as needed.

Any resubmittal attempts will be made first to your default Payment Method, and then to other Payment Methods you have on file. Once payment is authorized by an issuing bank on a Payment Method on file, all other resubmittals will be discontinued. If, after all resubmittals, payment is still not authorized by your issuing bank(s), your next scheduled order will be automatically canceled, and you will not receive that order.  Your next order after your canceled order will remain as scheduled and we will repeat this payment process for that and all subsequent orders.

2.5       Fees associated with your Subscription. A $6.99 fee applies to most orders below $35. You can add additional items or Subscriptions to your order to avoid this fee. A $9.95 store delivery fee also applies to orders with a scheduled delivery window, which is required for certain Subscriptions. This fee is waived for Walmart+ Members as a benefit of membership. Government-mandated fees may apply in certain locations. Any fees that apply to your order will be disclosed in a reminder communication sent prior to fulfillment.

2.6       Canceling, Skipping, and Editing Subscriptions and/or Specific Orders. We’ll send you an email several days before fulfilling a specific subscription order, to remind you of the delivery date and time, and to confirm the price of the product(s) being delivered pursuant to your Subscription enrollment. To avoid being charged for a specific order, you must cancel before the order is processed as indicated in your reminder email. To cancel, skip, edit, or change the delivery date or time for your Subscription, go to the My Subscriptions section of your account. Changes to your Subscription must be made prior to the date the order is processed or said changes may not be applied until the next fulfillment of your Subscription.

2.7       Product Eligibility for Service. Only Walmart Subscribe items identified by icons, buttons, links or other selectable features titled as “Subscribe” or other similar language are eligible for the Service.  Walmart reserves the right to remove certain items from eligibility for the Service at any time without notice.  If you select an item that becomes temporarily out of stock at the time of order placement or shipping, you authorize us to skip a subscription cycle on your behalf if a reasonable substitute cannot be identified. If you select an item that becomes permanently discontinued, you authorize us to cancel your item subscription on your behalf. Certain products may require authorization by a prescriber such as a veterinarian. If Walmart is not able to obtain authorization Walmart reserves the right to cancel the product subscription.

2.8       No Commercial Use of the Service. The Service may be used for personal, non-commercial use only, and benefits may not be shared, assigned, or transferred to any third party.

2.9        Substitutions. You agree that we may substitute a similar item in the event your Subscription cannot be fulfilled with the original selected item. You will be given the opportunity to reject said substitution prior to fulfillment.

      3.  Marketplace Program as Part of Walmart Subscriptions Service.Marketplace Retailers may participate in the Service. You may choose Subscriptions from Marketplace Retailers.

Each individual Promotional Program is subject to separate terms of use which govern your use of and participation in such program. The terms of use for Promotional Programs that are a benefit of Walmart+ Membership can be found in the Walmart+ Terms of Use. These Walmart Cash Offers Program terms of use govern how Walmart Cash may be redeemed, and are subject to the Walmart.com Terms of Use found here.

1.2 Walmart Cash has no cash, monetary or other value prior to being successfully redeemed pursuant to a valid distribution request in accordance with these Walmart Cash Offers Program terms of use at a Walmart store or through Walmart.com or the Walmart App. No interest is paid on Walmart Cash balances. Walmart Cash does not constitute property, and customers who have accumulated Walmart Cash have no ownership or other legal interest therein for any purpose. The use of the word “earn” in these terms of use or in the terms of use for any Promotional Program or other materials does not imply that Walmart Cash has any value prior to being successfully redeemed pursuant to a valid distribution request; nor does it imply that any amounts are owed to the participant.

Walmart Cash is provided to you solely by Walmart under the Walmart Cash Offers Program, which is offered and provided by Walmart at its discretion based on rebates and other promotions provided by Walmart, including rebates and other promotions sourced in the aggregate, indirectly, from manufacturers or suppliers of products or services sold at Walmart. By accepting these terms of use, you agree and acknowledge that your sole relationship in connection with the Walmart Cash Offers Program is with Walmart.

The maximum amount is established by Walmart in its sole discretion. Unless prohibited by law, Walmart reserves the right to require a mandatory redemption of Walmart Cash periodically in its sole discretion.

1.5 Walmart Cash is non-transferable and non-assignable, cannot be brokered, bartered or sold, and cannot be divided as part of a settlement, legal proceeding or death, or combined with Walmart Cash of any other participant in the program. Walmart Cash does not expire, but customers must have a Walmart.com account in good standing to both obtain and redeem Walmart Cash.

2. Redeeming Walmart Cash
Once you have obtained Walmart Cash pursuant to the relevant Promotional Program’s terms of use, your Walmart Cash balance will be reflected on your Account page at Walmart.com or in the Walmart App. In order to redeem your Walmart Cash, you may request: (i) to have your Walmart Cash applied against most purchases you make at a Walmart store or at Walmart.com, or (ii) to have your Walmart Cash redeemed for an equal amount of money either (a) to be deposited into a ONE debit account owned by you or (b) at a Walmart Money Center. The processes for these redemption methods are set forth below. Fully completing the process for a redemption method is the exclusive means of making a valid redemption request. Walmart reserves the right to add, delete or modify redemption options from time to time at its sole discretion.

2.1 Redemption for a Walmart eGift Card to be Applied Against Walmart Purchases
When you request to apply your Walmart Cash to an in-store or online purchase at Walmart, subject to other terms contained herein, we will digitally redeem your Walmart Cash for a Walmart eGift card that will be instantly applied against your basket total. In order to do so, please follow the steps below. eGift cards are subject to the standard Walmart Gift Card Terms and Conditions found here, except that eGift cards under this Walmart Cash Offers Program are provided to you by Walmart as loyalty, award and promotional products. At all times, eGift cards awarded in the Promotional Programs remain the property of Walmart.

In-Store:

Using the Walmart app, sign into your Walmart account where you have accrued a Walmart Cash balance.
At checkout scan the Walmart Pay QR code with the Walmart app on your phone.
Tap "Use Walmart Cash" to redeem your balance for an eGift card to be applied against the purchase price and select how you would prefer to pay for any remaining amount (if applicable). If your Walmart Cash balance is less than your basket total (excluding prohibited items), your full Walmart Cash balance will be redeemed for an eGift card and applied against your basket total. If your Walmart Cash balance is greater than your basket total, only that portion of your Walmart Cash balance necessary will be redeemed for an eGift card and applied against the basket total.
Online:

On Walmart.com or the Walmart app, sign into your Walmart account where you have accrued a Walmart Cash balance.
In checkout, tap “apply Walmart Cash balance" in the Wallet section to redeem your Walmart Cash balance for an eGift card to be applied against the purchase price and select how you would prefer to pay for any remaining amount (if applicable).
The eGift card cannot be used to purchase the following items:

All gift cards, including, but not limited to:
Walmart and Sam’s Club gift cards
Any 3rd party gift cards (e.g., Apple, Google, PlayStation, Starbucks)
Network-branded gift cards (i.e., Visa, Mastercard, AMEX gift cards)
Re-loadable prepaid cards (e.g., Green Dot, BlueBird)
Prescription-related products, including, but not limited to:
Prescription products such as prescription pet medications, prescription drugs, and prescription medical supplies
Prescription co-pays or deductibles
Health care services provided by Walmart Health or Walmart Health Virtual Care
Pharmacy purchases submitted to any health benefit program, pharmacy benefit program, insurer or state / federal government health care program
Optical products and services
Alcoholic beverages
Tobacco
Firearm and ammunition products
Lottery tickets
Financial services, including those offered through Walmart Money Centers (e.g., money transfers, money orders, bill payments)
Membership fees (Walmart+)
Tips paid to delivery drivers
Select third party services, as required by applicable law
YOU ACKNOWLEDGE AND AGREE THAT THE ITEMS THAT ARE ELIGIBLE FOR WALMART CASH OR EGIFT CARD REDEMPTION ARE DETERMINED SOLELY BY WALMART, AND THAT ALL EXCLUDED ITEMS MAY NOT BE FULLY-LISTED IN THESE TERMS OF USE. 2.2 Loyalty Digital Codes and Cash Out Redemption
Once you have accumulated at least $25 in Walmart Cash, you may request that all or a portion of your Walmart Cash balance be redeemed, in line with the disbursement limits, for an equal amount of money (a “Cash Out Payment”). You will not be able to request a Cash Out Payment if you have less than $25 in Walmart Cash.

We will accommodate your request for a Cash Out Payment by providing a single-use, loyalty digital code issued by Sunrise Banks, N.A., Member FDIC (a “Loyalty Digital Code”). You may only redeem your Loyalty Digital Code to transfer funds to a ONE debit account owned by you or at Walmart Money Center locations to receive cash.

Loyalty Digital Codes are subject to the Sunrise Banks Loyalty Digital Code Terms and Conditions found here. Loyalty Digital Codes are funded by Walmart and provided to you by Walmart as loyalty, award and promotional products. Until successfully redeemed by you, any funds on the Loyalty Digital Codes remain the property of Walmart. The cash out services for the Loyalty Digital Codes are provided by InComm Financial Services, Inc. (NMLS # 912772) and are subject to additional terms available here. The ONE debit account is issued by Coastal Community Bank, Member FDIC, under license by [Mastercard© International].

To request a Cash Out Payment in your ONE debit account, visit the [Rewards Center] in your Walmart App, and select “Redeem Walmart Cash to ONE”. To redeem your Loyalty Digital Code, if you have a linked ONE debit account, follow the instructions in your Walmart App. If you do not have a linked ONE debit account, you may either link your account or create an account by following the instructions in your Walmart App.

To request a Cash Out Payment at a Walmart Money Center, visit a Walmart Money Center and display your barcode, as generated in your Walmart App, to an associate to scan. Your barcode is your Loyalty Digital Code. To redeem your Loyalty Digital Code, you must provide a valid, government-issued photo identification to the Walmart Money Center associate within three (3) hours of issuance. The identification that you provide must match the profile that you created with Walmart. Walmart reserves the right to refuse any form of identification it deems not acceptable, or that does not otherwise meet the requirements of Sunrise Banks or InComm Financial Services. Loyalty Digital Codes not redeemed within three (3) hours of issuance will be cancelled and Walmart Cash equivalent to the value of such Loyalty Digital Code will be credited back to your Walmart Cash balance.

2.3 No Automatic Redemptions
You are responsible for redeeming your Walmart Cash. There are no automatic redemptions of Walmart Cash. If you do not make a valid distribution request in accordance with these Walmart Cash Offers Program terms of use at a Walmart store or through Walmart.com or the Walmart App, we are under no obligation to send you an eGift card or a Loyalty Digital Code. Absent a redemption request, the credits will remain in your Walmart Cash account and will not expire.

5. Purchase and Delivery. You may purchase Gift Cards at Walmart Stores, Sam’s Clubs, in-app, online at Walmart.com and SamsClub.com, and at authorized Resellers.

6. Acceptance Locations. Unless other restrictions apply, you may use Gift Cards at any Walmart or Sam’s Club in the U.S., Puerto Rico, in-app, and online at Walmart.com and SamsClub.com.

7. Redemption. Gift Cards are redeemable towards purchases of eligible goods and services. Eligible goods and services are subject to change without notice in Walmart’s sole discretion.

8. Saving to Your Account.You may save a total of 5 Gift Cards in your Walmart or Sam’s Club Account (collectively “Account”) at any time. Saving Gift Card information to your Account enables online use, it does not restrict the use of the physical card or remove the balance. 

9. Check Your Gift Card Balance and Transaction History. By saving a Gift Card to your Account, you may access the balance and transaction history. You may also check the balance by calling 1-888-537-5503 or online, here.

10. The Gift Card Balance is an Estimate. The balance you see online or receive by phone is an estimate only. In most cases, when you make a purchase online or in stores, the balance adjusts immediately. There may be occasions when the balance update is delayed.

11. Consolidation. You may not consolidate or combine multiple Gift Cards to purchase a new Gift Card. We do not consolidate or combine multiple gift cards into a single Gift Card.

12. In Cash Redemption. The law requires cash redemption if your Gift Card balance falls below a certain dollar amount ONLY in these jurisdictions: CA, CO, CT, HI, MA, ME, MO, NJ, NY, OR, PR, RI, VT, WA.

13. Customer Service. You may call Gift Card Customer Service at 888-537-5503 for assistance. Gift Card Customer Service is available Monday through Friday from 7:00 AM to 9:00 PM, excluding major holidays.

14. Bulk Gift Card Program. You may use Gift Cards as personal or business gifts. If you are ordering more than $1,000 in Gift Cards, please apply for a Bulk Gift Card Account at www.walmart.com/bulkgiftcards. Approval of a Bulk Gift Card Account is at Walmart’s sole discretion.

20. Disputes, Arbitration, and Applicable Law.

PLEASE READ THIS SECTION CAREFULLY. IT MAY SIGNIFICANTLY AFFECT YOUR LEGAL RIGHTS, INCLUDING YOUR RIGHT TO FILE A LAWSUIT IN COURT.



Arbitration Agreement.

EXCEPT FOR DISPUTES THAT QUALIFY FOR SMALL CLAIMS COURT, YOU AGREE THAT ALL DISPUTES ARISING OUT OF OR RELATED TO THESE TERMS AND CONDITIONS OR ANY ASPECT OF THE RELATIONSHIP BETWEEN YOU AND WALMART, INCLUDING YOUR PURCHASE, ACCEPTANCE, OR USE OF GIFT CARDS OFFERED OR SOLD BY WALMART OR THE WALMART ENTITIES, WHETHER BASED IN CONTRACT, TORT, STATUTE, FRAUD, MISREPRESENTATION, OR ANY OTHER LEGAL THEORY, WILL BE RESOLVED THROUGH FINAL AND BINDING ARBITRATION BEFORE A SINGLE NEUTRAL ARBITRATOR INSTEAD OF IN A COURT BY A JUDGE OR JURY. EXCEPT AS OTHERWISE PROVIDED IN THESE TERMS AND CONDITIONS, YOU AND WALMART AGREE THAT EACH IS WAIVING THE RIGHT TO SUE IN COURT AND TO HAVE A TRIAL BY A JURY. This paragraph may be referred to as the “Arbitration Agreement” in these Terms and Conditions.

Class Action and Mass Action Waiver.

YOU AND WALMART AGREE THAT ANY ARBITRATION REQUIRED BY THE ARBITRATION AGREEMENT WILL TAKE PLACE ON AN INDIVIDUAL BASIS. You and Walmart each agree to waive the right to have any dispute or claim subject to the Arbitration Agreement brought, heard, administered, resolved, or arbitrated as a class arbitration, class action, collective action, or Mass Action to the maximum extent permitted by law. “Mass Action” means a situation in which a party is represented by a law firm or other representative, or a collection of law firms or other representatives, that has initiated more than one hundred (100) arbitration Demands with common questions of law or fact against Walmart within 180 days of initiating your arbitration Demand. This paragraph may be referred to as the “Class Action and Mass Action Waiver” in these Terms and Conditions. Nothing in these Terms and Conditions precludes you from bringing issues to the attention of federal, state, or local government agencies and, if the applicable law allows, such agencies may seek relief against Walmart for you.

Initiating a Demand for Arbitration.

Any arbitration required by the Arbitration Agreement shall be initiated by You or Walmart by sending the other a written demand for arbitration (“Demand”) only via first-class mail, FedEx, or UPS within the applicable statute of limitations period. Your Demand shall be delivered to: Walmart Inc. Legal Department, 702 SW 8th Street, Mailstop 0215, Bentonville, AR 72716. The Demand must include (i) the name, telephone number, mailing address, and email address of the person or entity seeking arbitration; (ii) a statement of the legal claims asserted and the factual basis for those claims; (iii) a description of the remedy sought and an accurate, good-faith calculation of the amount in controversy; (iv) the original personal signature of the party seeking arbitration; and (v) the original personal signature of any legal counsel or other representative purporting to represent the party seeking arbitration. For purposes of this paragraph, “original personal signature” does not include any digital, scanned, electronic, copied, or facsimile signature. An original personal signature on the Demand certifies the following: (i) the Demand is not being presented for any improper purpose, such as to harass, cause unnecessary delay, or needlessly increase the cost of litigation; (ii) the claims and other legal contentions are warranted by existing law or by a non-frivolous argument for extending, modifying, or reversing existing law or for establishing new law; and (iii) the factual contentions have evidentiary support or, if specifically so identified, will likely have evidentiary support after a reasonable opportunity for further investigation or discovery. The original personal signature by the party seeking arbitration shall verify under penalty of perjury that the factual statements contained in the Demand are true and correct. Compliance with this paragraph tolls any applicable statute of limitations as to any dispute or claim subject to the Arbitration Agreement that is stated in the Demand.

Filing a Demand for Arbitration.

A party seeking arbitration of any dispute or claim subject to the Arbitration Agreement shall submit a copy of the Demand to the American Arbitration Association (“AAA”) sixty (60) or more days after the Demand is initiated pursuant to these Terms and Conditions. The arbitration will be administered by the AAA pursuant to the current AAA Consumer Arbitration Rules, except to the extent modified by these Terms and Conditions. The AAA rules and instructions are available on the AAA website at www.adr.org/consumer.

Conduct of Arbitration.

In any arbitration of a dispute or claim that is subject to the Arbitration Agreement:

1. You or Walmart may file such dispositive motions as would be permitted by the Federal Rules of Civil Procedure without obtaining permission from the arbitrator. Upon the filing of any dispositive motion, the arbitrator may stay all further action and deadlines in the arbitration until deciding such motion.

2. The parties shall have the right to conduct such discovery from the opposing party or any third party that is proportional to the needs of the claim or dispute, considering the importance of the issues at stake, the amount in controversy, the importance of the discovery in resolving the issues, and whether the burden or expense of the proposed discovery outweighs its likely benefit. Proportionality shall be decided by the arbitrator when requested by either party.

3. The arbitrator shall conduct any calls, conferences, or hearings by teleconference or videoconference, unless the arbitrator determines that an in-person hearing is appropriate. Any in-person hearing will be held at a location that is reasonably convenient. You and an employee of Walmart shall attend all arbitrator calls, conferences, and hearings.

4. A party may make a written offer of judgment at least seven (7) days before the date set for the arbitration hearing.

5. If the arbitrator finds that a party’s claim, counterclaim, or appeal was frivolous, asserted in bad faith, or pursued for purposes of harassment, the arbitrator shall award the opposing party its attorneys’ fees, costs, and expenses and all amounts charged by AAA for the arbitration.

6. The arbitrator will follow these Terms and Conditions and the law. The arbitrator shall not have the authority to commit errors of law or legal reasoning. The arbitrator may award relief only in favor of the individual party seeking relief and only to the extent necessary to provide relief warranted by that individual party’s claim. The arbitrator may not award relief for or against anyone who is not a party, though the individual relief awarded by the arbitrator may incidentally affect non-parties.

7. You or Walmart may appeal an arbitration award that manifestly disregards the law by filing a notice of appeal with the AAA within 30 days after an award is rendered and delivered to the parties. The appeal shall be heard by a single arbitrator unless the parties agree to a multi-arbitrator appellate panel. The arbitrator(s) assigned to the appeal shall be selected by agreement of the parties from among those on the AAA Appellate Panel. If the parties are unable to agree, AAA shall appoint the arbitrator(s) from among those on the AAA Appellate Panel. An arbitrator who previously presided over any aspect of a case shall be ineligible from serving as an appellate arbitrator in that same case. The appeal, including briefing, shall be conducted pursuant to the parties’ agreement or the appellate arbitrator(s)’ orders if the parties are unable to agree.

Process for Arbitration Bellwether Demands.

If twenty-five (25) or more Demands for arbitration of a dispute or claim subject to the Arbitration Agreement are initiated (i) that involve common questions of law or fact and (ii) where the initiating parties are represented by the same law firm, the same group of coordinated law firms, or the same representative, then such Demands shall be coordinated pursuant to this paragraph. The group of claimants and group of respondents shall each select five (5) Demands, for a total of ten (10), to be filed with AAA (collectively the “Bellwether Demands”). Each of the Bellwether Demands shall be assigned by AAA to a different arbitrator chosen from its national roster. After ninety (90) days from the date all Bellwether Demands have been arbitrated and any appeals exhausted, any remaining Demands subject to this paragraph may be filed with AAA. The AAA Supplementary Rules for Multiple Case Filings shall apply to the extent they are not inconsistent with this paragraph. Notwithstanding any other provision in the Terms and Conditions, You and Walmart agree that a court of competent jurisdiction shall have authority to enforce the terms of this paragraph with injunctive or other relief.

Severability.

If (i) any dispute or claim subject to the Arbitration Agreement is filed as a class arbitration, class action, collective action, representative action, or Mass Action and (ii) if the Class Action and Mass Action Waiver is held invalid or unenforceable as to such dispute or claim, then the Arbitration Agreement does not apply to that dispute or claim and such dispute or claim must be brought in a court of competent jurisdiction. If any provision of Section 20 of these Terms and Conditions other than the Class Action and Mass Action Waiver is held invalid or unenforceable as to a particular dispute or claim, then such provision shall be severed from the Terms and Conditions, and the remainder of the Terms and Conditions shall be enforceable to the maximum extent permitted by law.

Applicable Law.

The Federal Arbitration Act (“FAA”) governs the interpretation and enforcement of Section 20 of these Terms and Conditions. If the FAA is found not to apply to any portion of Section 20 of these Terms and Conditions, then the applicable laws of the State of Arkansas shall apply without regard to choice-of-law principles.

Ratings, Reviews, Question, & Answer Terms of Use


Guidelines for Submitting Ratings and Reviews
You can share your opinions on products you've purchased, or about Marketplace Sellers you’ve purchased from, on Walmart.com. Walmart employees can also submit reviews, which will be identified by a Walmart Associate label. Regardless of whether your experience with the product was positive or negative, your honest review will benefit other Walmart.com shoppers.

By submitting a review on Walmart.com, you (i) agree to our Privacy Policy﻿ and Terms of Use﻿, (ii) consent to any personal information in any Content (as defined in the Terms of Use, including for example, an image of your face or likeness, to be shared with Walmart affiliates anywhere in the world, and (iii) agree to comply with our ratings and reviews guidelines set forth herein, which we update from time to time.

Submit a Product Review
From the Purchase History page
Sign in to your Account
Navigate to the Purchase History page
Follow the prompt to review an item at the top of the Purchase history list
From the Order Details page
Sign in to Your Account
Navigate to the Purchase History page
Click on the “View details” button next to the order number for the product you wish to review
On the Order Details page, select “Review item” under the item name
From requests to review more items after you submit one review
From emails inviting you to review the products you purchased after your order is fulfilled; use the links provided to access the review forms
At the end of a post-purchase survey link printed on your in-store receipt
From the item’s page
Select Write a Review next to the Review Snapshot on the product details page
New reviews display on the website within 5-7 business days. If you have submitted a review recently, check back soon to see it posted to the site.

Submit a Seller Review
We offer two ways to submit a seller review:

From the Order Details page
Sign in to Your Account
Navigate to the Purchase History page
Click on the “View details” button next to the order number for the product you wish to review
On the Order Details page, select “Review seller” under the item name
After submitting an item review, you will be given the option to write a review for the seller that sold your Marketplace item
Product Review Guidelines
Please keep in mind the following guidelines when submitting a review for a product:

Reviews must be in English*
Reviews must reflect your honest opinion about the product
Reviews should focus on the product and your experience using it
Reviews should provide specific details about what you did or did not like about the product
Reviews must not include:
Content that is libelous, harassing, abusive, obscene, vulgar, sexually explicit or other similar language
Content that is discriminatory or prejudicial with respect to race, gender, sexuality, ethnicity, or other protected class
Content that is wholly unrelated to the product reviewed
Content considered as spam
Email addresses, phone numbers, links to websites, physical addresses, or other forms of contact information and personally identifiable information
Content that you do not own or do not have permission to use
In addition to the above guidelines, if you are submitting an image with your review, make sure:
The image is relevant to the product
The image does not infringe on any third-party intellectual property rights (including copyrights, trademarks, patents, trade secrets) or other proprietary rights
You have all intellectual property rights in the image, and permission to submit the image as part of the review
Please keep in mind the following guidelines when submitting a review for a Marketplace Seller:

Reviews must be in English*
Reviews must reflect your honest opinion about your experience purchasing from the Seller, including, for example, the customer service they provide
Reviews should provide specific details about what you did or did not like about your experience purchasing from the Seller
Reviews must not include:
Content that is libelous, harassing, abusive, obscene, vulgar, sexually explicit or other similar language
Content that is discriminatory or prejudicial with respect to race, gender, sexuality, ethnicity, or other protected class
Content that is wholly unrelated to the product reviewed
Content considered as spam
Email addresses, phone numbers, links to websites, physical addresses, or other forms of contact information and personally identifiable information
Content that you do not own or do not have permission to use
*Walmart reserves the right to translate submitted reviews into any other language.

Consequences for violations
If your review does not comply with the above guidelines, or if we find that you have engaged in any fraudulent, or other non-compliant activity, we reserve the right to:

Remove your reviews;
Not publish your review and you will not be able to submit a subsequent review for the same product;
Limit your ability to submit reviews;
Suspend or terminate your account
Reporting violations
To report suspicious seller activity related to reviews, please submit a report via our reporting form for reporting suspicious marketplace activity: Report Marketplace Seller Activity - Walmart.com.

To report a harmful review, please select the “Report” option available to the bottom right of each review on our website.

Frequently Asked Questions
Q: Can I edit or delete my posted review?
A: No, you are currently unable to edit or delete your item or seller reviews.

Q: Will my reviews be anonymous?
A: Your review will be posted under a nickname that you submit or, if no nickname is entered, it will be posted as “Walmart Customer”. We do not allow or publicize any personally identifiable information in reviews.

Q: Can I review a product that I have not purchased on Walmart.com?
A: No, reviews should only be written for products that you have purchased from Walmart.com.

Q: Can I review a seller that I have not purchased from on Walmart.com?
A: No, reviews can only be written for sellers that you have purchased from on Walmart.com.

Q: Can I rate a product without typing a written review?
A: Yes, you can rate a product by selecting the number of stars without writing a detailed review. However, providing detailed feedback is always appreciated.

2. About Your WWB Card

The WWB Card is a card that is being provided to you by your Health Plan, to purchase qualified items. The WWB Card may include access to multiple subaccounts, each containing funds issued by your Health Plan (with each such subaccount, a “Purse”).  WWB Card funds are issued by your Health Plan, not Walmart. Your Health Plan is responsible for ensuring funds are available to be loaded to the WWB Card. WWB Card funds may be issued or removed by the Health Plan, subject to Health Plan enrollment eligibility requirements.

Your Health Plan will provide you, or make available to you, documents that govern the types of items available to purchase with the WWB Card and any limitations on such purchases. Health Plan requirements and terms are not part of this Agreement. Contact your Health Plan for details about such requirements.

WWB Card funds are not your property.  You do not have the ability to add or load funds to the WWB Card. The WWB Card is not a gift card and is not connected in any way to a personal checking or savings account or any other financial account you may have. The WWB Card is not a credit card. You will not receive any interest on the funds associated with the WWB Card.

3. WB Card Activation

You must activate the WWB Card before it can be used. You may activate the WWB Card by visiting the Walmart web address provided by your Health Plan or by calling 1-833-316-1113 (TTY 711), hours of operation: M-F 8AM – 8PM ET. Your failure to activate and use the WWB Card results in the loss of all rights that you may have to use the WWB Card and access the underlying funds. By activating or using the WWB Card, you represent and warrant to us that you agree to only use the WWB Card in accordance with these Terms and Conditions.  You further represent and warrant to us that you understand these Terms and Conditions are binding.

4. Use and Redemption

WWB Cards may be used and redeemed at participating Walmart locations in the United States or in Puerto Rico, or online at Walmart.com, to purchase qualified items authorized by your Health Plan.  If you use the WWB Card without presenting your physical or digital WWB Card (i.e., mail order, telephone, or by Internet), the legal effect will be the same as if you presented the physical or digital WWB Card in person. WWB Cards are not redeemable towards previously purchased goods or services.  Except as may otherwise be required by law, the WWB Card cannot be: i) redeemed for cash; ii) used to obtain cash in any transaction; iii) used for illegal transactions; or, iv) used to set up recurring payments. Walmart may refuse to process any transaction for security reasons or if Walmart believes the transaction may violate the terms of this Agreement or applicable law.

Each time you use the WWB Card, you authorize Walmart to reduce the value available on the WWB Card or applicable Purse by the corresponding dollar amount of such qualified items, in addition to any applicable fees or charges. You are not allowed to exceed the available amount through an individual transaction or a series of transactions. Attempting to use the WWB Card when there are insufficient funds accessible with it may result in your transaction being declined or Walmart allowing you to pay for the balance of the transaction with another method of payment.

5. Authorized Use

 You are the only authorized user of the WWB Card. You do not have any rights in or to the funds accessible with the WWB Card, except the right to use the WWB Card and access the WWB Card funds in accordance with these Terms and Conditions. You are not authorized to use the WWB Card for any inappropriate, fraudulent or abusive transactions including, but not limited to: i) providing WWB Card access to any other individual; ii) using or attempting to use the WWB Card in any manner not approved in Health Plan documents relating to the WWB Card; or iii) using or attempting to use the WWB Card in violation of these Terms and Conditions.  6. WWB Card Balance Inquiry and Transaction History

 To check the balance or the transaction history on your WWB Card, please visit the Walmart web address provided by your Health Plan or call 1-833-316-1113 (TTY 711), hours of operation: M-F 8AM – 8PM ET. Any expired funds will be returned to the Health Plan and no longer available for use. Walmart is not responsible for replacing funds that may have expired.

Your WWB Card will be deactivated and any funds remaining on the WWB Card at the time of the request may be transferred onto a new WWB Card. If the funds are transferred onto a new WWB Card, the funds will be temporarily unavailable until the new WWB Card is activated. Your participation in the Program is also governed by our Walmart.com Terms of Use found here which incorporate our Privacy Policy. We may update these terms from time to time by posting updates on the Walmart.com website. By using this service, you accept the updated terms.

Participation in the Program allows you to register for the Program and tie your bank account information to your Walmart Account to initiate payment for purchase transactions at Walmart.com or SamsClub.com, which may be accessed on-line or through the Walmart App or Sam's Club App. In summary, this service allows you to initiate a transfer of funds directly from your bank account to pay for goods or services.

Eligibility. You are eligible to register for the Program if you are 18 years of age or older and you have an eligible bank account. The Agreement is in addition to any agreement you may have with your Bank. There may also be limits on the frequency and number of transactions that may be made by Pay by Bank.

Registration. To register for the Program, you must complete the Program Registration process. We reserve the right to deny your Program Registration, to reject your payment under the Program, and to terminate your ability to particpate in the Program for any reason and in our sole discretion. You may terminate your participation in the Program by deleting the Pay by Bank payment tender from your Walmart Account Payment Options page. You may also terminate your participation in the Program by calling your Bank to revoke authorization.

Authorization. This authorization applies to your Pay by Bank payment initiation that allows you to initiate a transfer of funds directly from your tied bank account to Walmart or a Walmart affiliate. By selecting the Pay by Bank payment tender, you authorize us to electronically debit your bank account up to the total amount of your purchase, including applicable taxes, fees, and shipping or delivery costs. You also authorize us to further debit or credit your bank account to correct any errors, make any necessary adjustments to your payment, or issue a refund back to your bank account.
