If you believe your privacy rights have been violated, you may file a written complaint, and there will be no retaliation, with our HIPAA Compliance Officer at the above address, or with the Secretary of the Dept. of Health and Human Services, Office for Civil Rights.







Política de Privacidad y Aviso sobre Prácticas de Privacidad de la Aplicación Wellness Services de Walmart Inc.

Última Actualización: Agosto 30 de 2022

Las actualizaciones incluyen:

Aclaraciones sobre nuestras prácticas de conservación
Agregado de una sección sobre cómo protegemos su información personal
Esta Política de Privacidad y Aviso sobre Prácticas de Privacidad de la Aplicación Wellness Services de Walmart Inc. (en adelante, la “Política de Privacidad”) cubre cómo y por qué recopilamos, utilizamos, compartimos y protegemos la información de salud recopilada dentro de la aplicación Walmart Wellness y los servicios incorporados. Toda la información de salud recopilada dentro de la aplicación Walmart Wellness se trata como información de salud protegida según se define en la Ley de Portabilidad y Responsabilidad del Seguro Médico (“HIPAA”).

Walmart Wellness, que es un término que abarca las distintas ofertas de salud y bienestar en línea provistas u organizadas por Walmart Inc. (“Walmart,” “nosotros” o “nuestro”), es un servicio en línea que ayuda a organizar, almacenar y administrar información relacionada con su atención o la de sus hijos, padres y seres queridos (los “Walmart Wellness Services”). Los Walmart Wellness Services se prestan a través de nuestra aplicación móvil a la que aquí denominamos “Walmart Wellness” (colectivamente, el “Sitio”).

Esta Política de Privacidad es una política complementaria a la Política de Privacidad Corporativa de Walmart y al Aviso sobre Prácticas de Privacidad de Walmart. Revise el Aviso sobre Prácticas de Privacidad de Walmart para conocer las prácticas sobre información específicas de la información del paciente que recopilan, utilizan, comparten y protegen distintas entidades de servicios de atención médica y asociados comerciales de Walmart. En caso de conflicto entre esta Política de Privacidad, la Política de Privacidad Corporativa de Walmart o el Aviso sobre Prácticas de Privacidad de Walmart, se considerará que esta Política de Privacidad será la que prevalezca en todos los aspectos relacionados con el procesamiento de su información por parte de Walmart Wellness Services. Walmart puede actualizar esta Política de Privacidad de tanto en tanto y lo notificará por medios razonables sobre la existencia de actualizaciones, e incluso publicará la fecha de la última revisión a través de la aplicación Wellness.

PERFILES, MIEMBROS Y CUIDADORES

Se requiere que tenga una cuenta de Walmart.com válida para registrarse con nosotros y poder acceder a Walmart Wellness Services y para convertirse en “Miembro” del servicio de Walmart Wellness. Como Miembro, podrá crear uno o más perfiles de atención (“Perfiles”) y añadir información, datos e imágenes a sus Perfiles (“Contenido del Perfil”). Un Perfil puede estar relacionado con usted o un hijo, padre u otra persona a su cuidado.

Walmart se toma muy en serio la protección de su información de salud privada, así como su capacidad para controlarla y acceder a ella. Walmart está obligada por ley a proteger la información que usted proporciona a través de Walmart Wellness Services en cumplimiento con todas las políticas de privacidad de Walmart aplicables. Las protecciones que ofrecemos y describimos en esta Política de Privacidad, así como las protecciones disponibles bajo las leyes de privacidad estatales y federales, se aplicarán a la recopilación, el uso y la divulgación de su información por parte de Walmart.

Sin embargo, como Miembro, tendrá la oportunidad de invitar a terceros cuidadores (“Cuidador”) a acceder a los Perfiles que crea. Tenga en cuenta que, por lo general, estas mismas protecciones no se aplicarán a los Cuidadores u otros que accedan a su Contenido del Perfil. Una vez que se otorga ese acceso, no controlamos el uso o la divulgación del Contenido del Perfil por parte de sus Cuidadores. Si decide invitar a un Cuidador para que tenga acceso a sus Perfiles, ese Cuidador también deberá tener una cuenta válida de Walmart.com y convertirse en Miembro.  Cuando establecemos el acceso de cada Cuidador al Contenido del Perfil, Walmart confiará exclusivamente en las instrucciones que nos brindó, proporcionadas a través de su cuenta, que indican que los Cuidadores tienen acceso al Contenido del Perfil debido a su participación en su atención de la salud o tratamiento, o por el pago de estos. Además, cualquier Cuidador que invite a sus Perfiles tendrá acceso total a todo el Contenido del Perfil dentro de sus Perfiles, además de la capacidad de añadir, eliminar, distribuir, descargar y editar el Contenido del Perfil.

RECOMENDAMOS ENCARECIDAMENTE QUE PIENSE CON DETENIMIENTO SI VA A PERMITIR EL ACCESO DE CUIDADORES A SUS PERFILES Y QUE CONTROLE CON FRECUENCIA EL ACCESO A SUS PERFILES. Todos los Miembros son responsables de controlar el Contenido del Perfil cargado o añadido a sus Perfiles, ya sea que este sea cargado por el Miembro, un Cuidador o un tercero. WALMART NO SE RESPONSABILIZA POR LA PRECISIÓN DEL CONTENIDO DEL PERFIL PROVISTO POR USTED U OTROS, Y NO PODEMOS EVITAR QUE SUS CUIDADORES DIVULGUEN CONTENIDO DEL PERFIL QUE USTED HUBIERA PUESTO A DISPOSICIÓN DE ESTOS. Si ya no desea dar acceso a su Perfil a sus Cuidadores, puede comunicarse con el equipo de soporte de Wellness para dar por finalizado el acceso.

Si crea un perfil en nombre de otra persona, se requiere que acepte los Términos de Uso de los Servicios de Walmart Wellness y acepte que está autorizado por esa persona a crear un perfil en su nombre. En la medida en que realice acciones en nombre de la persona a la que brinda cuidados, también certifica que tiene autoridad legal válida para realizar acciones en su nombre.

TIPOS DE INFORMACIÓN RECOPILADA

Es posible que recopilemos las siguientes categorías de información suyas cuando utiliza Walmart Wellness. Toda la información recopilada de usted por parte de Walmart Wellness estará protegida y será procesada de acuerdo con los requisitos legales y las reglamentaciones de implementación relacionadas con las entidades cubiertas y los asociados comerciales que manejan información de salud protegida regulada por la HIPAA. No todas las categorías de información se recopilan para todos los individuos. Toda información recopilada a través de Walmart Wellness se manejará de acuerdo con los estándares establecidos en esta Política de Privacidad y el Aviso sobre Prácticas de Privacidad de Walmart. La información recopilada por Walmart Wellness Services puede incluir, entre otras cosas, lo siguiente:

identificadores personales, como nombre y dirección;
identificadores de dispositivos y en línea e información relacionada, como número de teléfono y dirección de correo electrónico;
actividad en internet, aplicaciones y en redes, como cookies y páginas visitadas;
identificadores gubernamentales, como números de identificación nacional y de licencia de conducir;
información demográfica, como edad y fecha de nacimiento;
información financiera, como números de tarjeta de crédito y débito e información sobre reclamos;
información protegida de salud y sobre seguros médicos, como números de recetas y números de identificación del seguro médico; imágenes de documentos con información de salud e información derivada de tales documentos;
características de categorías protegidas por leyes estatales o federales, como género y nacionalidad;
información sobre el historial de compras, lo que incluye productos de cuidado de la salud comprados a entidades cubiertas de Walmart y asociados comerciales;
información de ubicación, como geolocalización;
preferencias y características individuales, como deducciones relacionadas con los patrones y comportamientos de compra.
USOS Y DIVULGACIONES DE INFORMACIÓN

Walmart Wellness puede procesar o compartir la información que suministre a través de Wellness Services para los siguientes fines sin tener que antes solicitarle autorización explícita:

Para facilitar la prestación o coordinación de tratamiento para atención de la salud, lo que incluye, entre otras cosas, entregar servicios o medicamentos recetados, y para facilitar tratamiento eficaz por parte de proveedores de atención de la salud que participan en su tratamiento o cuidado.
Para facilitar el pago de servicios de atención de la salud, como facilitar verificaciones de elegibilidad para programas estatales de Medicare o Medicaid.
Para la administración eficaz de operaciones de atención de la salud de entidades cubiertas de Walmart, asociados comerciales y otras entidades cubiertas con las que tenga una relación de tratamiento.
Para las operaciones de salud colectivas de proveedores de salud afiliados a Walmart. Tenga en cuenta que cada uno de los proveedores de atención de la salud de entidades cubiertas que participan en su acuerdo de atención de la salud organizado pueden compartir información de salud entre sí a fin de realizar operaciones de atención de la salud relacionadas con el acuerdo de atención de la salud organizado. Específicamente, los proveedores asociados y las prácticas de Walmart Health pueden compartir información de salud protegida, originada de Wellness Services, entre sí y con Health & Wellness de Walmart o Sam’s Club para este propósito.
Para comunicarse con usted sobre productos y servicios relacionados con su tratamiento de atención de la salud, lo que incluye, entre otras cosas, recordatorios de citas, información de atención preventiva, servicios de manejo de terapia de medicamentos o tratamientos alternativos.
Para permitir que los Cuidadores administren o participen de su atención o en el pago de esta. Podemos divulgar su información a un cuidador o representante autorizado que usted nos indique si entendemos razonablemente, basado en sus interacciones con este a través de Wellness Services, que la divulgación es apropiada para facilitarle su atención o el uso de Wellness Services.
Divulgaremos información relacionada con su uso de Wellness Services si así lo requieren las leyes locales, estatales o federales.
Divulgaremos información de ser necesario para evitar una amenaza de servicio a la salud o seguridad de una persona en particular o el público.
Podremos divulgar información a una agencia de control de la salud para actividades autorizadas o requeridas por la ley.
Podremos divulgar información a autoridades gubernamentales para fines de actividades de seguridad y salud pública, como la prevención o el control de enfermedades, lesiones o discapacidades, denunciar abuso, negligencia o violencia doméstica e informar retiros de productos o reacciones adversas a medicamentos.
Podremos divulgar información en respuesta a órdenes administrativas o judiciales, o en respuesta a una citación, solicitud de producción de pruebas, u otro proceso jurídico si se hicieron los esfuerzos necesarios para informarle a usted sobre la solicitud o para que obtenga una orden de protección.
Podremos divulgar información a un funcionario de cumplimiento de la ley para ciertos fines de cumplimiento de la ley, como denunciar un delito en nuestras instalaciones o responder a indagaciones legítimas de cumplimiento de la ley.
Podremos divulgar su información para respaldar funciones gubernamentales especializadas: (1) si usted es miembro de las fuerzas armadas, según lo soliciten las autoridades del mando militar; (2) si usted es un recluso o está bajo custodia policial, a una institución correccional o funcionario de cumplimiento de la ley; (3) en respuesta a una solicitud de cumplimiento de la ley, bajo ciertas condiciones; (4) por motivos de seguridad nacional autorizados por la ley; (5) a funcionarios federales autorizados para proteger al Presidente, otras personas autorizadas o jefes de estado extranjeros.
Podremos divulgar su información de salud personal (PHI) para la compensación de trabajadores o programas similares.
También podremos divulgar su PHI a organizaciones de obtención de órganos o similares a fines de una donación o trasplante.
Podremos divulgar información al director de una funeraria, un médico forense o examinador médico de acuerdo con la ley aplicable para permitirles llevar a cabo sus funciones.
Podremos divulgar su información a una persona autorizada legalmente a actuar en su nombre, como un padre, tutor legal, administrador o albacea de su patrimonio.
Wellness Services ofrece maneras de almacenar y compartir datos sobre inmunizaciones. Podremos brindar prueba de inmunización a una escuela sobre un alumno o posible alumno, según lo requiera la ley, si el padre/tutor, menor emancipado u otro individuo autorizado así lo autorizan, según corresponda. También podremos brindar maneras de compartir datos sobre inmunización con otras aplicaciones y servicios autorizados, así como a los Cuidadores con acceso a su Perfil.
Walmart Wellness puede procesar o compartir la información que suministre a través de Wellness Services para los siguientes fines solicitando previamente su autorización explícita:

No utilizaremos ni divulgaremos su información a cambio de una remuneración directa o indirecta, a menos que usted nos autorice a hacerlo o según esté permitido por la HIPAA. Podremos intercambiar información cuando esta esté anonimizada de acuerdo con el método de determinación de expertos de la HIPAA o el método de puerto seguro de la HIPAA.
Excepto en circunstancias limitadas y según lo permita la ley, no podremos utilizar o divulgar sin su autorización notas registradas por un profesional de salud mental que documenten su conversación durante una sesión de terapia.
Con su autorización, podremos divulgar su información con fines de marketing.
Podremos utilizar o divulgar información con fines de investigación con su autorización, o sin autorización en circunstancias limitadas como con una exención de una junta de revisión institucional. A excepción de lo descripto, no utilizaremos ni divulgaremos su PHI sin su autorización. Puede cancelar su autorización por escrito en cualquier momento, lo que detendrá todo uso o divulgación futuros para los fines cubiertos por su autorización, excepto cuando ya hayamos actuado según su permiso.
INFORMACIÓN OBTENIDA A TRAVÉS DE UN DISPOSITIVO ASOCIADO CON USTED

Cuando utiliza un teléfono o una computadora para acceder a Wellness Services, nos está brindando información de forma pasiva a través de la tecnología. Esta información ayuda a que nuestros sitios web y servicios móviles funcionen y nos permite comprender las necesidades de nuestros clientes y proporcionar información sobre nuestros productos y servicios que pueda ser de su interés. Podremos recibir lo siguiente:

Información de Dispositivos: Recopilamos información técnica cuando visita nuestras aplicaciones o servicios móviles. Esto puede incluir información como la dirección MAC, la dirección de Protocolo de Internet (IP), el tipo de dispositivo móvil que utiliza, el sistema operativo y tipo de navegador de su dispositivo, un identificador único de dispositivo, la dirección de los sitios web de referencia, la ruta de navegación en nuestros sitios web e información sobre su sesión en nuestros sitios web.
Información de Navegación: Utilizamos tecnologías propias y de terceros como cookies, balizas web (web beacons) e identificadores de dispositivos móviles para recopilar información sobre el uso de nuestros sitios web y servicios móviles. Utilizamos estas herramientas para proporcionarle una experiencia de compra integral y más personalizada. Por ejemplo, las cookies nos permiten proporcionarle información relevante a medida que utiliza nuestros sitios o regresa a ellos; las balizas web (web beacons) nos permiten saber si se visitó una página específica, si se abrió un correo electrónico o si los anuncios publicitarios en nuestros sitios web y otros sitios fueron eficaces.
Información Precisa sobre la Ubicación de los Dispositivos: Podemos recopilar información sobre su ubicación cuando su dispositivo está configurado para proporcionar información sobre la ubicación. Por ejemplo, la señal de GPS de sus dispositivos nos permite mostrarle la ubicación más cercana donde puede obtener Wellness Services en persona. Otros ejemplos son permitirnos saber cuándo está por llegar a una cita de atención de la salud programada en una ubicación de Walmart Health o facilitar el proceso de ingreso cuando llega. Puede ajustar la configuración de ubicación de su dispositivo móvil en cualquier momento para controlar si el dispositivo comunica esta información. También deberá ajustar la configuración de Bluetooth del dispositivo para deshabilitar completamente la recopilación de datos relacionados con la ubicación por Bluetooth. Consulte las instrucciones de su dispositivo para obtener más información sobre estos ajustes. Además, podemos ofrecerle controles sobre nuestro uso de su información de ubicación móvil. Sin embargo, tenga en cuenta que cambiar nuestro acceso a estos ajustes tendrá un impacto en su capacidad de usar algunos de los servicios de Wellness Services.
Cámara, Medios y Micrófono: Wellness Services puede solicitarle que tome o suba fotografías o videos de usted, su imagen, sus registros de salud, recetas, resultados de exámenes, códigos QR u otra información a fin de almacenar sus datos dentro de Wellness Services, facilitar el acceso a la información en su cuenta y acceder a servicios de atención médica. Wellness Servicies solicitará acceso a su cámara o fotografías almacenadas cuando sea necesario. Nunca accedemos o cargamos medios almacenados de su dispositivo si no los ha seleccionado para cargarlos. No utilizaremos la cámara de su dispositivo sin avisarle previamente. Wellness Services también puede requerir acceso al micrófono para interactuar con proveedores de atención de la salud que utilicen Wellness Services a fin de proporcionar servicios de atención de la salud a usted y otros pacientes. No utilizaremos el micrófono de su dispositivo sin avisarle previamente. Por último, Wellness Services puede acceder al almacenamiento de su dispositivo para recopilar información que se utilizará para completar el Contenido del Perfil, lo que incluye información sobre su seguro y medicamentos. No utilizaremos información almacenada en su dispositivo para completar el Contenido del Perfil sin avisarle antes.
Lista de Contactos: El servicio de Wellness puede solicitar información almacenada en la lista de contactos de su dispositivo a fin de compartir y almacenar la información de contacto de familiares, amigos u otras personas que usted haya identificado como que requieren privilegios de acceso a su información médica con el objeto de prestar servicios de atención de la salud o pagar por estos. Esta información de contacto también puede utilizarse para solicitar acceso a la información de salud de otras personas que usted pretende actúen como un Cuidador. Nunca accedemos a información ni la cargamos desde la lista de contactos de su dispositivo a menos que usted seleccione contactos a utilizar o cargar. No utilizaremos la lista de contactos de su dispositivo sin avisarle antes.


DERECHOS SOBRE SU INFORMACIÓN

Cuenta con los siguientes derechos respecto de su información:

Puede solicitar restricciones sobre el uso o la divulgación de su información para operaciones de atención de la salud, pago o tratamiento, o nuestra divulgación de su PHI a alguien involucrado con su atención o el pago de esta, como un familiar o amigo. No estamos obligados a aceptarlo. Si lo aceptamos, cumpliremos con su solicitud excepto en ciertas situaciones de emergencia o según lo exija la ley.
Puede solicitar restricciones respecto de la divulgación de su información a su plan de salud con el objeto de efectuar un pago u operaciones de atención de la salud para servicios pagados en su totalidad (de su bolsillo).
Puede inspeccionar y recibir una copia en papel o electrónica de sus registros médicos, si esta es fácil de producir. Por lo general, esto incluye registros de facturación y recetas. Podremos cobrarle los costos razonables en los que se incurra para responder a esta solicitud. Podremos denegar su solicitud; en ese caso, puede solicitar una revisión de la denegación.
Puede solicitar que corrijamos la información si está incorrecta o incompleta. Debe indicar un motivo que respalde su solicitud. Podremos denegar su solicitud si la información es correcta y está completa o si no forma parte de la información conservada para o por el proveedor relevante según nuestro acuerdo de atención de la salud organizado. Si denegamos su solicitud, tiene derecho a presentar una declaración de desacuerdo. Su solicitud pasará a formar parte de su registro médico, y se incluirá cuando divulguemos el punto o la afirmación que usted cree está incompleto o incorrecto.
Puede solicitar que se rinda cuenta de las divulgaciones de su información. Se trata de una lista de las divulgaciones de su información, que no hayan sido realizadas para operaciones de atención de la salud, pago o tratamiento, y otras excepciones permitidas por la ley. Su solicitud debe especificar un período, que no puede ser mayor de seis años a partir de la fecha de la solicitud.
Puede solicitar que nos comuniquemos con usted de cierta manera o en cierta ubicación. Por ejemplo, puede solicitar que solo nos comuniquemos con usted en su trabajo, en una residencia distinta o a través de una casilla de correo. Su solicitud por escrito debe indicar cómo y dónde desea que nos comuniquemos. Las solicitudes razonables serán otorgadas. Si desea ejercer cualquiera de estos derechos, comuníquese con la ubicación de Walmart o Sam’s Club que le prestó los servicios para obtener el formulario correspondiente o envíe una solicitud por escrito a HIPAA Compliance, Walmart Inc., 2608 SE J Street, Mailstop 0230, Bentonville, AR. 72716-0230. Puede obtener una copia de esta Política de Privacidad, y los formularios relacionados, comunicándose con el equipo de soporte de Wellness Services a http://walmart.com/help. Sin embargo, tenga en cuenta que para utilizar Wellness Services con el objeto de recibir comunicaciones, debe aceptar cumplir con los Términos de Servicio de Wellness Services siempre que utilice Wellness Services.
ELEGIBILIDAD Y PROHIBICIÓN RESPECTO DEL USO DE PARTE DE NIÑOS

Walmart Wellness Services son servicios en línea para el público en general diseñados solo para personas mayores de 15 años. Queda expresamente prohibido el acceso o uso de Walmart Wellness Services por parte de personas menores de 15 años. Con el acceso o uso de Walmart Wellness Services usted declara y garantiza ser mayor de 15 años. Cuando establecemos el acceso de cada Cuidador al Contenido del Perfil, Walmart confiará exclusivamente en las instrucciones que nos brindó, proporcionadas a través de su cuenta, que indican que los Cuidadores tienen autorización y son elegibles para acceder al Contenido del Perfil y utilizar Walmart Wellness Services.

TECNOLOGÍA Y ENLACES DE TERCEROS

Walmart Wellness Services pueden contener enlaces a recursos o sitios web de terceros, lo que incluye enlaces a recursos o sitios web operados por proveedores de atención de la salud, como entidades profesionales que prestan servicios dentro de ciertas ubicaciones de tiendas minoristas de Walmart conocidas como “Walmart Health” y, potencialmente, otros proveedores de atención de la salud. Tenga en cuenta que ni Walmart, ni Walmart Wellness Services, administran o controlan directamente: (i) la disponibilidad o precisión de esos recursos o sitios web; o (ii) el contenido, los productos o servicios en o disponibles de esos recursos o sitios web. Los enlaces a esos recursos o sitios web no implican el respaldo de Walmart de esos recursos o sitios web o del contenido, los productos o los servicios disponibles de esos recursos o sitios web, o las políticas de privacidad o prácticas de los operadores de los mismos.

Toda tecnología o servicio de terceros provistos, disponibles, vinculados o accesibles de otro modo a través de Wellness Services se proporcionan solamente para su conveniencia y no están bajo nuestro control. Walmart no respalda, recomienda ni declara o garantiza de otro modo ninguna tecnología de terceros.  Antes de utilizar los servicios provistos por un tercero, disponibles a través de Wellness Services, lo alentamos enfáticamente a revisar las políticas de privacidad y los términos de uso correspondientes de esas tecnologías de terceros.

AVISOS

Todo aviso u otra comunicación permitida o requerida en virtud del presente, incluso aquellos sobre modificaciones a estos Términos, se realizarán por escrito y Walmart las entregará: (i) por correo electrónico (en cada caso a la dirección que nos indique); o (ii) mediante su publicación en el Sitio. En el caso de los avisos enviados por correo electrónico, la fecha de recepción será la fecha en que el aviso fue transmitido.

¿POR CUÁNTO TIEMPO CONSERVAMOS SU INFORMACIÓN PERSONAL?

Conservaremos la información personal que recopilamos de usted durante el tiempo necesario o adecuado para llevar a cabo los propósitos establecidos en esta Política de Privacidad y cualquier otro aviso proporcionado en el momento de recopilación de los datos, como para proporcionarle productos o servicios, apoyar nuestras operaciones y funciones comerciales, comunicarnos con usted y realizar investigaciones, o cumplir con nuestras obligaciones contractuales o legales.  Cuando así se requiera, eliminaremos la información recopilada cuando ya no se necesite para los propósitos descriptos en esta Política de Privacidad o cualquier otro aviso proporcionado en el momento de recopilación de los datos, de acuerdo con los procedimientos y las políticas de conservación de Walmart.



¿CÓMO PROTEGEMOS SU INFORMACIÓN PERSONAL?

Reconocemos la importancia de mantener la seguridad de la información personal de nuestros clientes. Contamos con un equipo de asociados responsables de ayudarnos a proteger la seguridad de su información. Ya sea que utilice nuestros servicios móviles o en nuestras tiendas, tomamos medidas de seguridad razonables incluidas protecciones físicas, administrativas y técnicas. Estas medidas pueden incluir controles de acceso de seguridad físicos y técnicos u otras protecciones, políticas y tecnologías de seguridad de la información, procedimientos para garantizar la correcta eliminación de la información y programas de capacitación.

Para obtener más información sobre las medidas que puede tomar para proteger la seguridad de su información personal, haga clic aquí.

MÁS INFORMACIÓN O PARA INFORMAR UN PROBLEMA

Si tiene preguntas sobre esta Política de Privacidad, comuníquese con HIPAA Compliance, Walmart Inc., 2608 SE J Street, Mailstop 0230, Bentonville, AR. 72716-0230 o por teléfono al (800) WAL-MART. Si cree que se infringieron sus derechos de privacidad, puede presentar una queja por escrito, sin miedo a sufrir represalias, ante nuestro Director de Cumplimiento de la HIPAA a la dirección anterior, o ante el Secretario del Departamento de Salud y Derechos Humanos, Oficina de Derechos Civiles.



Claims of Intellectual Property Infringement


IP Infringement Claims
Walmart respects the intellectual property (“IP”) rights of others and has set up the Brand Portal and the online IP Claim Form for owners of IP rights to report legitimate claims of infringement as to items listed on Walmart.com, including claims of copyright, trademark, patent, publicity and counterfeit. Walmart strives to promptly process and investigate such claims and take appropriate actions under applicable laws.

To make a claim of IP infringement, you will be required to declare, under penalty of perjury, that the information provided is correct and that you are the IP rights owner or an authorized agent. To understand what constitutes infringement, continue reading. To make an IP claim now, visit the Brand Portal or fill out the online IP Claim Form. If the information you submit is inaccurate or incomplete, we may be unable to process or respond to your request. If you have questions about IP or whether your rights have been infringed, consider seeking legal advice.

Copyright Claims
Copyright infringement is the unauthorized use of a creative work that is protected under state and/or federal copyright law. Examples of works protected under copyright law include photographs, videos, artwork and music. Pursuant to the Digital Millennium Copyright Act, Title 17, United States Code, Section 512(c)(2) (the “DMCA”), notifications of claimed copyright infringement should be made to Walmart’s “Designated Agent." You may notify Walmart's Designated Agent by using the Brand Portal or the IP Claim Form. Copyright protected content can be removed and/or disabled if it was utilized or disseminated by Walmart.com without proper permission from the copyright owner. Under the DMCA, claimants who make misrepresentations concerning copyright infringement may be liable for damages (including costs and attorneys' fees) incurred by the alleged infringer or by Walmart, if injured by relying upon such misrepresentation in removing or disabling access to the material or activity claimed to be infringing.

If you received a DMCA notification and believe it was submitted in error, you may submit a counter notice to request that your listing be republished. The counter notice must contain all of the following information: 1) Contact Information – include name, email address, physical address and telephone number; 2) URL or other identifying information of material to be republished; 3) A statement under penalty of perjury that you have a good faith belief that the material was removed or disabled as a result of mistake or misidentification of the material to be removed or disabled; 4) A statement that you consent to the jurisdiction of Federal District Court for the judicial district in which your address is located and will accept service of process from the person who provided the DMCA notification or an agent of such person; and 5) A physical or electronic signature. Counter notices should be sent to the following email address: ipin﻿vest@walmart.com﻿.

Trademark Claims
A trademark is a word, phrase, symbol or design that identifies and distinguishes the source of goods or services of one party from those of others. Under United States trademark law, a trademark owner has the right to prevent competitors from using its trademark if the use would cause a likelihood of confusion. If you believe that your registered trademark has been infringed, please notify Walmart by using the Brand Portal or the IP Claim Form. Note that Walmart does not mediate trademark disputes between third parties and trademark owners. Rather, Walmart will forward the trademark claim to the third party before taking any action, with the intent that the third party and trademark owner will resolve between themselves any dispute over ownership rights and infringement. Walmart reserves the right to remove any content that is found to infringe the rights of a trademark owner.

Counterfeit Claims
Counterfeit goods are inauthentic items that are intended to appear authentic. If you believe that a counterfeit of your product is accessible or being sold on Walmart.com, please notify us by using the Brand Portal or the IP Claim Form. Select the counterfeit reporting option. Walmart reserves the right to remove any content it believes to be counterfeit and further reserves the right to take any additional measures it deems appropriate in handling a claim of counterfeit goods.

Patent Claims
A patent is a grant from the United States Patent Office that gives the holder exclusive right to its article of manufacture. If you believe that your patent has been infringed under United States patent law, please notify Walmart by using the Brand Portal and the IP Claim Form. Note that Walmart does not mediate patent disputes between third parties and patent owners. Rather, Walmart will forward the claim to the third party with the intent that the third party and patent owner will resolve between themselves any dispute over ownership rights and infringement. Walmart reserves the right to remove any content that is found to infringe the rights of a patent owner.

Repeat Infringer Policy
Walmart takes appropriate action against parties it categorizes as repeat infringers. A repeat infringer includes, but is not limited to, anyone who qualifies as such under the DMCA or who receives multiple claims of IP infringement and such claims are determined by Walmart to be valid. Action may include, but is not limited to, removal of listings and suspension or termination of any relationship with Walmart.

What is Not Enforced
Although a manufacturer may enter into an exclusive distribution agreement with a seller of a product, violations of such an agreement are not the subject of IP infringement. Under the IP laws of the United States, rights owners may lose their rights to prevent the further sale of a product after the “first sale” by the manufacturer. Therefore, the enforcement of exclusive distribution agreements is a matter between the manufacturer and its retailers, and Walmart will not take action to enforce such agreements.

Walmart Cash FAQs


Last Updated January 10, 2025

Walmart Cash Offers Program General FAQs
What is the Walmart Cash Offers Program?
Walmart Cash is promotional currency available to customers with a Walmart account through certain programs offered by Walmart from time-to-time.  One such program is through Manufacturer Offers sourced by Ibotta. Walmart+ members can also get Walmart Cash through other membership programs, including exclusively by booking through the Walmart+ Travel benefit. Members and customers can redeem Walmart Cash toward future Walmart purchases in-store and online, or for cash-out in store (limitations apply).

See Full Terms﻿ for an explanation of how the Walmart Cash Offers Program works.

How do I get Walmart Cash?
All customers with a valid Walmart.com account can get Walmart Cash by using the manufacturer offers promotional program. Walmart+ members can also get Walmart Cash with the Walmart+ Travel promotional program, and other programs that may be offered from time-to-time.

See below for how the promotional programs work.

Is Walmart Cash actually cash?
No, Walmart Cash are loyalty, award or promotional credits that have no cash, monetary or other value prior to being successfully redeemed at Walmart in connection with future purchases in stores or online, or for cash out in-store (see “How does Walmart Cash redemption work?” for details on how redemption works).

How can I check my Walmart Cash balance?
On Walmart.com or in the Walmart app, navigate to your account. Your current Walmart Cash balance will be on your account page.

In addition, the Walmart Cash page will show a summary of your Walmart Cash balance, as well as the total amount of Walmart Cash you have received as a participant in the program. Select the “Ways to Earn / Earn more” link from your account to visit the Walmart Cash page and then select “View your Walmart Cash activity” to view balance details and activity.

Does Walmart Cash expire?
No.

Is Walmart Cash a credit card?
No.

How do I redeem my Walmart Cash balance?
Once you’ve accumulated Walmart Cash, you can redeem it as follows:
In-store

Using the Walmart app in store, sign into your Walmart.com account.
At the register or self-checkout, scan the on-screen QR code to connect your account. To scan you may use either the scanner in the Walmart app located in the search bar, Walmart Pay, or if you have the Walmart app downloaded, you can also scan with your native phone camera.
Tap "Use Walmart Cash" to redeem your current balance for a Walmart e-gift card to be applied against the purchase price and select how you would prefer to pay for any remaining amount (if applicable). If your Walmart Cash balance is less than your basket total (excluding prohibited items), your full Walmart Cash balance will be redeemed for an e-gift card and applied towards your basket total. If your Walmart Cash balance is greater than your basket total, only that necessary portion of your Walmart Cash balance will be redeemed for an e-gift card and applied towards the basket total.
Online

On Walmart.com or the Walmart app, sign into your Walmart account.
In checkout, tap “Apply” next to your Walmart Cash balance in the Payment Method section to redeem your balance and select how you would prefer to pay for any remaining amount (if applicable).
Cash out in-store

Using the Walmart app or Walmart.com on your phone, sign into your Walmart account.
Within your account, tap “How to get / Get more” to visit the Walmart Cash page.
On the Walmart Cash page in app, tap “Cash out in-store” in the Walmart Cash tools section. On the Walmart Cash page on web, go to the “Redeem Walmart Cash”.
You will need a minimum balance of $25 Walmart Cash to use this option.
Tap “Show cash out barcode” to generate a cash out barcode unique to you. Your barcode represents a single-use, Loyalty Digital Code issued by Sunrise Banks N.A., Member FDIC, that is instantly redeemable for cash. Loyalty Digital Codes are subject to the Sunrise Banks Loyalty Digital Code Terms and Conditions found here. Your unique barcode will expire 3 hours after your barcode is generated. A new barcode can be generated following the steps above.
Head to the MoneyCenter or customer service desk at any Walmart store.
Display your barcode to the associate to scan, as well as your valid, government-issued photo identification (e.g., your driver’s license or state-issued ID) for security reasons. The name on the identification presented to a Walmart associate must match the name on your Walmart+ account. Your barcode will then be redeemed for cash. See the Walmart Cash Terms of Use for additional information.
To cash out in-store, you will need a minimum of $25 Walmart Cash. The maximum amount you can redeem for cash in a single day is $500, in a single week is $1,000, and in a single month is $2,000. The maximum amount you can redeem for in-store or online purchases, or a combination of cash and in-store or online purchases, is $2,000 per day.

How does Walmart Cash redemption work?
Walmart Cash are loyalty, award or promotional credits that have no cash, monetary or other value prior to being successfully redeemed pursuant to a valid distribution request. What's a valid distribution request? That just means the action you take to redeem Walmart Cash. While the process for redemption occurs seamlessly in the Walmart App or Walmart.com in the backend a few things happen when you make a request to redeem Walmart Cash:

For future purchases instore or online, a Walmart e-gift card is loaded, which can be applied against most purchases you make at a Walmart store or at Walmart.com.
For in-store cash out, a single-use, Loyalty Digital Code is issued by Sunrise Banks N.A, Member FDIC, which will be instantly redeemable for cash at a Walmart Money Center upon presentation of a valid, government-issued photo ID and the Loyalty Digital Code barcode. The name on the identification presented to a Walmart associate must match the name on your Walmart account. Other than presentation of an appropriate form of identification at a Walmart Money Center, no additional action is required by you as the process for redemption occurs seamlessly when you make a request to redeem Walmart Cash. The cash out services for the Loyalty Digital Codes are provided by InComm Financial Services, Inc. (NMLS# 912772).
How much available Walmart Cash do I need before I can redeem it?
There is no Walmart Cash balance minimum to redeem in connection with purchases in Walmart stores or Walmart.com.

Are there products or services that cannot be purchased using Walmart Cash?
Walmart Cash can’t be redeemed in connection with purchases for the following items or categories of products, both in-store and online:

Alcoholic beverages
Tobacco
Firearm ammunition products
Lottery tickets
Financial services, including those offered through Walmart Money Centers (money transfers, money orders, bill payments)
Membership fees (Walmart+)
All gift cards, including, but not limited to:

Walmart and Sam’s Club gift cards
Any 3rd party gift cards: e.g. Apple, Google, PlayStation, Starbucks, etc.
Network-branded gift cards (i.e., Visa, Mastercard, AMEX gift cards)
Re-loadable prepaid cards (e.g. Green Dot, BlueBird)
Prescription-related products, including, but not limited to:

Prescription products such as prescription pet medications, prescription drugs, and prescription medical supplies
Prescription co-pays or deductibles
Health care services provided by Walmart Health or Walmart Health Virtual Care
Pharmacy purchases submitted to any health benefit program, pharmacy benefit program, insurer or state / federal government health care program
Optical products and services
Walmart reserves the right to determine or change the products or services for which Walmart Cash is made available for redemption, without notice to you.

Do I lose my Walmart Cash balance if I cancel my Walmart account or Walmart+ membership?
No.

What happens to my Walmart Cash if I pause my Walmart+ membership?
Walmart Cash is available to all Walmart customers regardless of their Walmart+ membership status.

Promotional Program: Manufacturer Offers
What are manufacturer offers?
All customers with a valid Walmart.com account can get Walmart Cash through manufacturer offers on eligible items.

When you’re visiting Walmart.com or using the Walmart app, eligible items will display the amount of Walmart Cash associated with a relevant product and any purchasing requirements. These manufacturer offers will be identified with a checkbox labeled “Get Walmart Cash” or similar terminology.

Once you claim a manufacturer offer and the associated product is purchased, delivered or picked up, Walmart will add the value of the specific offer to your Walmart Cash balance. You can redeem this balance on future purchases in Walmart stores or Walmart.com, or cash it out in-store. See Full Terms.

Who is eligible to get Walmart Cash on manufacturer offers?
All customers are eligible. To participate in the program, you need a Walmart.com account.

How do I get manufacturer offers?
Sign into your Walmart account on Walmart.com or the Walmart app.
Find and select the “Get Walmart Cash” box associated with individual products on Walmart.com or in the Walmart app. You can find manufacturer offers from the Walmart Cash page, search bar or product page.
Tap "Get Walmart Cash" to claim a manufacturer offer and save it to your account.
In store, you can also find and claim manufacturer offers using the barcode scanner in "Store mode" in the Walmart app.
Purchase the item with the manufacturer offer. Your Walmart Cash will be added to your account when the item is purchased in- store, picked up or delivered.

Where can I see all my saved manufacturer offers?
You can see all your saved manufacturer offers on the Walmart Cash page in the “Your claimed offers” section. You can get to this page on Walmart.com or in the Walmart app by navigating to your account and selecting the “Ways to Earn / Earn more” link.

How do I get Walmart Cash on manufacturer offers?
In-store

Using the Walmart app in store, sign into your Walmart account.
At the register or self-checkout, scan the on-screen QR code to connect your account. To scan you may use either the scanner in the Walmart app located in the search bar, Walmart Pay or if you have the Walmart app downloaded, you can also scan with your native phone camera.
Purchase the associated item.
Once you have completed the transaction and purchased the associated item, the value of the relevant manufacturer offer you selected will be added to your Walmart Cash account. Nothing further is required from you.
Online

On Walmart.com or the Walmart app, sign into your Walmart account where you have claimed manufacturer offers.
Add the eligible item to your cart.
Place your order.
Once your order is delivered or picked up from the store, the value of the relevant manufacturer offer you selected will be added to your Walmart Cash balance. Nothing further is required from you.

What if I forget to scan the QR Code in-store?
If you forget to scan the Walmart Pay QR Code at checkout, you can still earn Walmart Cash on manufacturer offers if you scan your receipt barcode within 7 days of purchase. We’ll check to make sure you haven’t already received Walmart Cash on that transaction, and that you clipped the offer before checking out. Once confirmed, we’ll credit your account with the relevant Walmart Cash value of the manufacturer offer you selected. To do this:

Download the Walmart app and sign into your Walmart account.
In the search bar, select the barcode icon. You can submit up to 5 receipts in a 24-hour period.
Scan the barcode on your store receipt.
To redeem your Walmart Cash balance in-store, you need to scan the Walmart Pay QR code at the register prior to completing the purchase.

How long does it take for Walmart Cash to show up in my balance?
It may take a few minutes and up to 24 hours for the manufacturer offer value to be reflected in your Walmart Cash balance after your in-store transaction. For online orders, Walmart Cash will be reflected in your Walmart Cash balance after the order is delivered or picked up. Your Walmart Cash balance can be found in your Walmart account Wallet and the Walmart Cash page.

Why did I not receive Walmart Cash for an item?
There are a few reasons why you may not receive Walmart Cash for an item:

The item was out of stock, so the purchase of the item could not be completed.
The item you selected was unavailable and had to be substituted with another item that was not eligible for the manufacturer offer.
The manufacturer offer was not correctly saved or added to your account prior to purchase.
The manufacturer offer had expired prior to purchase. See Offer Details for expiration dates.
Do manufacturer offers expire?
Yes, manufacturer offers have expiration dates and the associated item must be purchased before that expiration date. Manufacturer offers that have expired will be removed from your saved manufacturer offers on the Walmart Cash page. See Offer Details for expiration dates.

Can I get and redeem Walmart Cash in the same purchase?
Yes. You can get Walmart Cash on manufacturer offers and redeem your existing Walmart Cash balance on the same transaction. However, the Walmart Cash you receive in that transaction can only be applied towards future transactions.

Can I use paper coupons and receive Walmart Cash on manufacturer offers for the same item?
No. If you use a paper coupon in-store and have also added a manufacturer offer to your account, the paper coupon will be honored, and you will not get Walmart Cash on the manufacturer offer for that item in that purchase.

Do I forfeit the Walmart Cash I received if I return an item?
When an item for which you received Walmart Cash through a manufacturer offer is returned and the purchase price is refunded to you, the Walmart Cash for that item will be reversed and deducted from your balance, unless there is another item in your transaction to which the original manufacturer offer could be applied.

If your Walmart Cash balance is not sufficient to cover the deduction, your balance will be negative. You will not be able to redeem any future earned Walmart Cash until you reach a positive balance.

If the item with the manufacturer offer I selected is substituted or out of stock, will I still receive Walmart Cash?
No. Only substituted items that also qualify will earn Walmart Cash.

Promotional Program: Walmart+ Travel
Where can I find my booking(s)?
Check your email inbox for a confirmation sent by Expedia. Additionally, you can access your booking(s) by going to WalmartPlusTravel.com and selecting Trips.

How is the Walmart Cash calculated when booking vacation packages?
If you’re booking a combination of air travel and other accommodations, your earnings will be calculated based on 2% Walmart Cash on air travel and 5% Walmart Cash on everything else.

What happens to my Walmart Cash if I cancel or modify my reservation(s)?
The Walmart Cash you earn with the corresponding booking will be adjusted in your “pending” Walmart Cash balance within 24-48 hours in your account.

Will I earn Walmart Cash through Walmart+ Travel if I cancel my membership?
No, you must be a current Walmart+ member at the time of booking to earn Walmart Cash; you cannot earn Walmart Cash retroactively. If you’ve booked your trip prior to cancelling your membership, you will receive the Walmart Cash earned for that booking 30 days after you have completed that booking.

Can I redeem my Walmart Cash on future bookings through Walmart+ Travel?
No, you can only use your Walmart Cash on future Walmart purchases or redeem for cash back. See Walmart Cash FAQs for questions on restrictions for redeeming Walmart Cash.

When will I see my Walmart Cash earned through Walmart+ Travel in my account?
You will see your Walmart Cash as “pending” in your account until your travel is complete. The final amount of Walmart Cash you earned will be reflected in your account 30 days after the completion of your trip or reservation.

Will the Walmart Cash earned through Walmart+ Travel be combined with my other earning programs?
Yes, all the Walmart Cash that you earn will be pooled into one balance and can be redeemed on future purchases in Walmart stores, Walmart.com, or redeemed for cash back at any Walmart store. See Walmart Cash FAQs for questions on restrictions for earning Walmart Cash.

Do I earn loyalty points for Expedia or other loyalty sites?
If you book through Walmart+ Travel, you earn 2% Walmart Cash on air travel and 5% Walmart Cash on everything else. Walmart+ Travel purchases are not eligible for points from other loyalty programs.

If I have any questions, changes, or issues with my trip or bookings, who can I contact?
You can contact Expedia customer service at +1(800)323-3897 (toll free) or +1(312)279-7755 (international toll) for additional assistance with your reservations. You can also contact the airline directly for specific questions regarding your flight. For questions about your Walmart Cash balance, you can reach us at Walmart.com/Help.

Why am I not seeing the correct amount of Walmart Cash I should be getting reflected on the Walmart+ Travel site (WalmartPlusTravel.com)?
Please log out and log back into WalmartPlusTravel.com to refresh and resolve the issue.



Walmart Pet Services Privacy Notice


Last modified: September 26, 2023

What Does This Privacy Notice Cover?
Walmart Inc. (“Walmart” or “we” or “us”) respects your privacy and are committed to protecting it through our compliance with this privacy notice. Walmart is not a veterinarian or provider of other pet services. Walmart provides its customers with the ability to obtain veterinarian or other pet services (collectively, the “Pet Services”) by independent practitioners, including, but not limited to, services provided by Community Veterinary Clinics LLC (“PetIQ”). The term “Pet Services” also include information we may collect when you make an appointment online to request Services. Walmart does, however, control the means and purposes of PetIQ’s and Walmart’s processing of the personal information collected about you (and your pet) when you use the Pet Services and is responsible for providing you with this Privacy Notice. By using the Pet Services, you agree and acknowledge that PetIQ or other independent practitioners may provide us with Personal Information related to your use of the Pet Services and that we may use such Personal Information as described in this Privacy Notice.

This Privacy Notice (our “Privacy Notice”) describes the types of information we may collect, use, maintain, protect, disclose, or otherwise process about you when you use the Pet Services and our practices for collecting, using, maintaining, protecting, disclosing, or otherwise processing that information.

This policy applies to information we collect:

on or through your use of the Pet Services;
in email, text, and other electronic messages between you and Walmart or PetIQ related to your use of the Pet Services through any medium;
when you interact with our advertising related to the Pet Services on third-party websites, if those applications or advertising include links to this policy; and
any other information we may collect about you offline when you use the Pet Services.
It does not apply to information collected by:

us through any other means, including on any other website or other products and services we offer unrelated to the Pet Services operated by Walmart or PetIQ or any third-party (including our affiliates and subsidiaries). For example, this Privacy Notice does not apply to any Personal Information we may collect and use related to your other purchases in our stores, or when you use other services such as eyewear or clinical services. For more information about our use of your Personal Information that we collect in these circumstances, please see the appropriate privacy notice at: https://corporate.walmart.com/privacy-security;
us or any of our affiliates or subsidiaries related to your or any other individual’s employment or potential employment with us. For more information about the Personal Information we collect related to employment or potential employment with us, please see the appropriate privacy notice at: https://corporate.walmart.com/privacy-security; or
any third party (including our affiliates and subsidiaries), including through any application or content (including advertising) that may link to the Pet Services.
Please read this Privacy Notice carefully to understand our policies and practices regarding your information and how we will treat it. If you do not agree with our policies and practices, your choice is not to use Pet Services. By accessing or using Pet Services, you agree to this Privacy Notice. This Privacy Notice may change from time to time (see Changes to Our Privacy Notice). Your continued use of our Pet Services after we make changes is deemed to be acceptance of those changes, so please check this Privacy Notice periodically for updates.

How Does Walmart Protect the Privacy of Children Related to the Services?
Our Pet Services are not intended to be used by children under 13 years of age. No one under age 13 may provide any information to us regarding the use of the Pet Services. We do not knowingly collect Personal Information from children under 13. If you are under 13, do not register for or otherwise provide any information to use the Pet Services, including your name, address, telephone number, email address, or any screen name or user name you may use. If we learn we have collected or received Personal Information from a child under 13 without verification of parental consent, we will delete that information. If you believe we might have any information directly from a child under 13, please contact us at the contact information below.

What Personal Information Do We Collect and How Do We Collect It?
Throughout this Privacy Notice, the term “Personal Information” means any information that identifies, relates to, describes, references, is reasonably capable of being associated with, or could reasonably be linked, directly or indirectly, with a particular individual or device. However, Personal Information does not include any deidentified or aggregated information.

Generally

We collect Personal Information from various sources, including:

directly from you when you provide it to us;
information that we create about you as you use the Pet Services; and
From third parties, for example, other veterinarians or providers of other pet services.
Information You Provide to Us

We collect the following types of Personal Information directly from you when you register for or use Pet Services: real name, postal address, email address, telephone number, emergency contact information, pet name, pet type (dog, cat, fish), pet breed, pet gender, pet birthday, pet veterinarian, current pet health and medical history. In addition, we also collect other types of information that you may provide when you fill out a form or through your correspondence with us.

We collect Personal Information from you when you use our Pet Services through:

Forms in our facilities or online when you register or book an appointment to receive the Pet Services. We may also ask you for information when you report a problem with the Pet Services;
if you contact us, records and copies of your correspondence (including email addresses);
your responses to surveys that we might ask you to complete for research purposes; and
details of the services you receive when you use our Pet Services and fulfillment of your requests (such as medications for your pet, vaccinations, and other procedures).
Information We Create About You

We may also create certain information about you. When we associate this information with other Personal Information about you, we consider this information to be Personal Information. This information includes: records of services obtained or other purchasing or consuming histories or tendencies related to the Pet Services and genetic characteristics, physiological characteristics, behavioral characteristics, biological characteristics, predispositions, and behavior of your pet.

Information We Collect from Third Parties

We may collect Personal Information from third parties. When we associate this information with other Personal Information about you, we consider this information to be Personal Information. This information includes: medical information and history, age, birthdate, color, breed, and gender of your pet.

How Do We Use Personal Information?
We use information that we collect about you or that you provide to us, including any Personal Information:

to provide you with and personalize the Pet Services that you request from us;
to support, develop, troubleshoot, and debug the Pet Services;
to create, maintain, customize, and secure your Pet Services account with us;
to provide you with notices about your account, such as recommended treatments and upcoming vaccinations that are due;
to provide you with support and to respond to your inquiries, including to investigate and address your concerns and monitor and improve our responses;
to help maintain the safety, security, and integrity of the Pet Services and our business;
for internal testing, research, analysis, and product development, including to develop and improve the Pet Services;
detecting incidents, protecting against malicious, deceptive, fraudulent, or illegal activity, and prosecuting those responsible for that activity;
to carry out our obligations and enforce our rights arising from any contracts entered into between you and us, including for billing and collection;
to notify you about changes to the Pet Services;
in any other way we may describe when you provide the information;
to fulfill any other purpose for which you provide it; and
for any other purpose with your consent.
We may also use your Personal Information to contact you about other goods and services related to the Pet Services that may be of interest to you. If you do not want us to use your Personal Information in this way please contact us at the contact information below. For more information, see Choices About How We Use and Disclose Your Information.

Who Do We Disclose Personal Information To and Why?
We do not share, sell, or otherwise disclose your Personal Information for purposes other than those outlined in this Privacy Notice. However, we may disclose aggregated information about our users, and information that does not identify any individual, without restriction.

We may disclose Personal Information that we collect or you provide as described in this Privacy Notice:

to our subsidiaries and affiliates. Such use by our subsidiaries and affiliates are governed by their applicable privacy notice, which may be found at: https://corporate.walmart.com/privacy-security.
to contractors, service providers, and other third parties we use to support the Pet Services and who are bound by contractual obligations to keep Personal Information confidential and use it only for the purposes for which we disclose it to them. These entities provide veterinary and other pet services or related services, such as shipping and receiving, lab testing, and other similar services;
to your regular veterinary or other veterinary or pet services upon your request;
to a potential or actual buyer or other successor in the event of a planned or actual merger, divestiture, restructuring, reorganization, dissolution, or other sale or transfer of some or all of Walmart’s or PetIQ’s assets, whether as a going concern or as part of bankruptcy, liquidation, or similar proceeding, in which Personal Information held by Walmart or PetIQ about the Pet Services’ users is among the assets transferred;
to fulfill the purpose for which you provide it. For example, if you give us the contact information about your current or previous veterinary, we will use your Personal Information to obtain your pet’s previous medical history or otherwise discuss your use of the Pet Services with them;
for any other purpose disclosed by us when you provide the information; and
with your consent.
We may also disclose your Personal Information:

to comply with any court order, law, or legal process, including to respond to any government or regulatory request;
to enforce or apply Pet Services terms of use and other agreements, including for billing and collection purposes; and
if we believe disclosure is necessary or appropriate to protect the rights, property, or safety of Walmart, our customers, your pet, or others. This includes exchanging information with other companies and organizations, such as a local animal welfare agency, for the purposes of protecting your pet from you.
What Choices Do You Have About How We Use and Disclose Your Information?
We do not control the collection and use of your information collected by third parties described above in Disclosure of Your Information. When possible, these organizations are under contractual obligations to use this data only for providing the services to us and to maintain this information strictly confidential. These third parties may, however, aggregate the information they collect with information from their other customers for their own purposes.

In addition, we strive to provide you with choices regarding the Personal Information we have collected about you. We have created mechanisms to provide you with control over your Personal Information:

Promotional Offers from Walmart. If you do not want us to use your contact information to promote other related products and services, you can opt-out by contacting us at the contact information below or, if we have sent you a promotional email, clicking the unsubscribe link in the body of that email. This opt out does not apply to information provided to Walmart as a result of your use of the Pet Services or your experience when using the Pet Services.
How Can You Access, Delete, and Update Your Personal Information?
You may request access to, or request that we correct or delete any Personal Information that we have collected about you by contacting us at the contact information below. We cannot delete your Personal Information except by also deleting your account. We may not accommodate a request to change or delete your Personal Information if we believe the change or deletion would violate any law or legal requirement or cause the information to be incorrect.

How Do We Secure Your Personal Information?
Walmart and PetIQ have implemented measures designed to secure your Personal Information from accidental loss and from unauthorized access, use, alteration, and disclosure.

Although we do our best to protect your Personal Information, we cannot guarantee the security of your Personal Information that we collect when you use our Pet Services. Any provision of Personal Information is at your own risk. We are not responsible for circumvention of any privacy settings or security measures related to the Pet Services.

How Will You Know if This Privacy Notice Changes?
We may change this Privacy Notice at any time. It is our policy to post any changes we make to our Privacy Notice on this page with a notice that the Privacy Notice has been updated at the point of the Pet Services (in the store, or online if you make an appointment for the Pet Services online). If we make material changes to how we treat our users’ Personal Information, we will notify you by email to the email address specified in your account and/or through a notice where you use the Pet Services (in the store or online if you make an appointment for the Pet Services online). The date this Privacy Notice was last revised is identified at the top of the page. You are responsible for ensuring we have an up-to-date active and deliverable email address for you, and for periodically visiting this Privacy Notice to check for any changes.

Your continued use of the Pet Services following the posting of changes constitutes your acceptance to such changes.

How Can I Contact Walmart?
If you have any questions, concerns, complaints, or suggestions regarding our Privacy Notice or the ways in which we collect and use your Personal Information described in this Privacy Notice, have any requests related to your Personal Information pursuant to applicable laws, or otherwise need to contact us, you may contact us through the Store & Corporate Feedback page and select “Company Feedback and Questions.” Alternatively, you can write us at:

Walmart Corporate Privacy

702 SW 8th Street, MS #0160

Unclaimed Property Letter Sample Text:
Our records indicate we are in possession of the property subject to your State of residence’s (as shown above) unclaimed property law.

If you wish to claim these funds and prevent this property from being turned over to the State as required by law: Please sign, print current address, include one copy of Proof of Ownership (see Frequently Asked Questions on the back of this letter for a list of accepted documents) and return to our office by xx/xx/xx. If any of these steps are not completed by the date previously listed, this property will be remitted to the State. You will then need to contact the State’s Unclaimed Property Division directly to claim your property.

Not everyone will see these tags.
If you buy one of these items, you must submit a review within the specified time to get the reward promo code.
You must review the items after they're delivered to ensure that the reviews reflect your honest feedback about your experiences with the item.

Please keep in mind these guidelines when leaving a review for a Marketplace Seller. Clear and specific feedback ensures your review is posted for others’ benefit.

Reviews for Marketplace Sellers must be submitted in English and meet all of the following:

Focus solely on your experience with the seller, including
Delivery time
Shipping methods
Customer service
Remember the purpose of submitting a seller review is to provide insight for other customers who are considering purchasing from the same seller.
Give details about what you did or didn't like about your experience.
Comply with our Terms of Use.

Any information not related to the Marketplace Seller
Obscenities
Offensive language

Advertisements
Content considered as spam
References to other products
Offers

Critical or spiteful comments about other reviews or reviewers
Reviews with no direct experience purchasing from the seller

Walmart Marketplace Promise
This promise gives you peace of mind when buying items sold and shipped by our third-party sellers.

The promise applies in the following cases:

Two or more days have passed since the expected delivery date, and you haven't received the item.
If you request a refund, we accept requests for 30 days after the last estimated delivery date of the item you ordered.
Tracking shows the item hasn't shipped to the shipping address.
You received items(s) different than expected and the seller refused the return.
Your item returned with a trackable shipping method and the seller hasn't issued you a refund within 2 business days of the receipt.
The seller has taken the cost of return shipping fees
The return was at the fault of the seller.
Walmart will contact the seller and give them the chance to make it right. The seller has 2 business days to find a satisfactory resolution. If you’re not satisfied, Walmart will step in and find the right final solution.

Intellectual Property
If you're an authorized representative of a registered brand and if you believe there is a counterfeit product or other infringing content on Walmart.com, please notify us by using the Intellectual Property (IP) Claim Form or the Brand Portal. Authorized representatives of a Brand Portal registered brand may ask the brand owner to sign up for an account and invite you as a user.

Stolen Goods
The sale of stolen property violates the property rights of rightful owners and is illegal. Walmart strictly prohibits the sale of stolen goods on the marketplace. If you believe there is stolen property listed for sale on Walmart.com, please notify us using the Report Seller Form and report the activity to your local law enforcement agency. 

Suspected criminal activity involving stolen goods can be reported to the California Office of the Attorney General using the Organized Retail Theft Webform. 

Suspicious Marketplace Seller Activity
If you see other unusual activity on Walmart Marketplace, please notify us using the Report Seller Form.

Marketplace Item Conditions – Automotive


Marketplace sellers must list the condition of the automotive items they’re selling on Walmart.com when they create a listing.

Items Listed as New
New: Brand-new, unused, unopened, undamaged item in the original packaging.
Must be factory sealed, with tags (and includes warranties and instructions for appropriate categories).
New without Box (or) Tags: Brand-new, unused item without the original packaging or tags.
Original accessories are included.
Instructions may not be included.
New with Defects: A brand-new, unused working item that has cosmetic defects caused during production or delivery.
This could include missing threads, discoloration, label malfunction, etc.
Instructions may not be included.
Items Listed as Remanufactured
A rebuilt automotive part that has been completely disassembled, cleaned, and examined for wear and breakage.
Worn out, missing, or non-functioning components have been replaced with new or rebuilt components.
It is the functional equivalent of a new part and is virtually indistinguishable from a new part that meets or exceeds original equipment (OE) specifications.
Instructions are included for appropriate categories.
Items Listed as Open Box
Excellent condition with no signs of usage and should still be in its original packaging.
Packaging can be open or show signs of wear, but original accessories should also be included.
Instructions are included for appropriate categories.
Items Listed as Restored
Like New: “Like-new” condition with very light micro-scratches, invisible at a distance of 8-12 inches (arm’s length). 
Battery life, if applicable, must be 80% of the “like-new” equivalent or higher. At least 90-days free returns. 
Good: The item has slight signs of wear/use, including small scuffs, marks, or scratches barely visible when holding the product 12 inches away, and imperceptible to touch.
Battery life, if applicable, must be 80% of the “like-new” equivalent or higher.
At least 90-days free returns.  
Fair: Item may have visible signs of wear/use, including more significant scratches or marks visible when holding the product 12 inches away, and perceptible to touch.

Preparing for Your Major Appliance Delivery


Walmart uses RXO as a delivery partner to deliver and set up appliances bought through Walmart Marketplace.

Stress-Free Delivery Starts Here
You’ll get a text or phone call with a 4-hour delivery window the evening before your scheduled delivery.

Pre-delivery Checklist
An adult (18 years of age, or older) must be present for the delivery.
Measure all doorways, hallways, and stairways along the delivery path to ensure they’re wide enough for the appliance. The delivery team needs an unobstructed path at least 10 feet wide to make the delivery. RXO can remove doors when needed.
Measure the space for your appliance and review the appliance specifications on the product page to confirm it fits.
Remove all belongings from the old appliance.
Prepare the Delivery Area
Confirm there are no more than 3 flights of stairs to the installation area (unless an elevator is available).
Clear the indoor path to the installation spot. Remove rugs, furniture, and personal items.
Make sure the outdoor delivery path is free from snow and/or other obstacles.
Secure and Reliable Setup
The RXO delivery team will:

Unpack, inspect, and prepare new appliance(s) for service.
Install new appliance(s) in regulation to manufacturers specification.
Depending on local code, the delivery agent may need to schedule a second visit to complete the installation.
Level the appliance.
Test the appliance to ensure it functions and inspect it for any visible damage.
Before accepting delivery, please inspect major appliances (such as refrigerators, washers, dryers, ranges, dishwashers, and certain microwaves) for any defects or damage.

If you discover any defects or damage after accepting delivery, you may still return the product—just report the issue within 48 hours by reaching out to the seller via the Walmart app or Walmart.com. Please refer to our full returns policy for additional information.

Disposal and Debris Removal
The delivery team will remove and dispose of all packaging from your new appliance. If you selected the haul-away service, they’ll also move or take away your old appliance.



Appliance Delivery Frequently Asked Questions


What steps should I take to ensure the installation goes smoothly?
Ensure you have measured, and your appliance fits in the required space. Make sure the unloading zone is clear and easily accessible, with no obstacles blocking the installation area. Additionally, an adult, 18 years or older, must be present at the time of delivery.

What should I do if my new appliance is damaged or not functioning properly upon delivery?
Ensure you inspect and test the appliance before the delivery agent leaves. If you spot any damage or if the appliance isn’t functioning properly, address the issue with the delivery agent right away. You must request a return within 48 hours of delivery. If you notice any defects or damage after the 48-hour return window, please refer to the manufacturer's warranty.

What could lead to my delivery getting delayed or rescheduled?
For a successful installation, ensure that all electrical outlets, water and gas shutoff valves, and cabinetry meet appliance requirements and comply with local codes.

Are built-in appliances included in installation?
Certain appliances are eligible for built-in installation but not all.

Can the delivery agents connect gas appliances to propane hookups?
No, delivery agents can’t connect gas appliances to propane. Agents aren’t licensed to install liquid propane or convert gas appliances to propane.

What if I become unavailable for my delivery?
Ask RXO to reschedule your delivery.
